(Also: surface climbs took twenty minutes more of the run, waiting in shelters eight.)
360. *mid-231-j, three hours less ten and no Nether; ended on a flip between the cast and the portal step.* The night mine took 37 minutes of the run, the food search 18, surface climbs 20. One stall is fixed: a staircase up a mountain toward (-730, 162, 69) found "no tool for snow block" on four of its steps and rested ten minutes. A block soft enough to dig by hand in a moment (hardness under one: snow, dirt, sand and the like) is now dug on a staircase without its tool, and the staircase's digs no longer ask for the drop; the room is what a stair wants.
361. *mid-205-k, "slain by Zombie" at minute 80, eleven seconds after a restart.* The bots were restarted for note 360 while this one held a pillar at y -8 among zombies; it rejoined in the same crowd with nothing decided, fell to y -10 and was killed in eleven seconds. It is the fifth death of the day to follow a restart by seconds (a boat twice, notes 338 and 357; a span, 353; a hoglin, 358; this). A new build is now taken at a quiet moment: the restart is asked for by touching .bot-state/restart-requested, and each bot quits for it once nothing hostile is within sixteen blocks and it stands on dry ground, off any span or seat, or after five minutes anyway (quiet-restart.js); the supervisor starts the new build as before.
362. *mid-235-j, "fireballed by Ghast" at minute 85, sixteen minutes into its Nether (reached at 69).* A minute after a restart, the fortress search stood at the end of its span at y 41 over the lava sea with no blocks left to build with (sixteen gravel, two planks); a ghast thirty-seven blocks off shot, the walls (note 332) could not go up for want of blocks, and it held still five times until the fireball threw it off, eleven blocks into the lava. With a shot or a biter pressing on a span and no walls to be had, the bot now steps back off the span to firm ground within eight blocks (an emergency, as the step out of lava is).
363. *mid-241-h, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 43, on a ledge at y 74.* At 8.9 health, hunting at night, the bot came onto a one-wide ledge; a creeper walked up, the span's rule held it still and crouched, and the creeper went off beside it. Walls do not stop a blast, and holding still is where a creeper lights. With a creeper coming within twelve blocks on a span, the bot now steps off it first, to firm ground at least two blocks farther from the creeper than it stands (note 362's step off, pointed away).
364. *mid-205-l, "suffocated in a wall" at minute 6.* Twenty seconds after rejoining (a quiet restart), the bot stood at (-368.9, 67, 577.5) in an open two-high cell and took a point of suffocation every half second for eight seconds, twenty to none, nothing dug. The game tests suffocation with a box round the eye four fifths of the body's width, and at x -368.9 that box reached into gravel in the next column (x -370); the bot's test looked at the eye's own cell only, which was open. The test is now the game's: every cell the eye's box touches, and the solid one found is the one dug out.
365. *mid-207-g, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 84 (not explained).* On a pillar of its own two blocks up at (25, 72, 296), a skeleton seven to twelve blocks off shooting every three seconds: the charge was chosen (set aside, no firm ground toward it from the pillar top), then the retreat twice, told a way was found ten blocks off, 1.8 seconds at a run. The retreat's walks stalled three times, three seconds each, forward held, sneak not, and the body hardly moved (velocity 0.02 to 0.07 a tick) from a pillar top with open air on every side; eleven health to none. What held the walk is not in the record: the crouch was off; a raised shield slows a walk to a fifth, and the shield state is not recorded. The flight record's keys now include "shield" while the shield is raised.
366. *mid-227-j, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 67 (not explained).* Thirty-eight seconds after a quiet restart, pillaring up toward its portal at y 103 from (-151, 64, -623), the work stopped for a creeper four blocks off (20:59), and in the three seconds before it went off nothing was reported by the survival layer: no stance asked, no back-off, one navigation that stalled at the pillar's foot (toward (-153, 66, -624), whose it is not in the record). Twenty to none at point blank. Why the survival layer made no answer to a creeper at four blocks is not in the record; watched for.
367. *mid-211-l, "shot by Pillager" at minute 44, by a stream (not fixed).* A pillager patrol, four crossbows six to eight blocks off, arrows about once a second: the rule's charge ran once, Jev chose a pillar and then a retreat (neither went: the bot was in and out of the water, swimming across), and by 5.5 health only the pocket and keeping on were left on offer, every other stance having failed here in the last twenty seconds. Sealed, it was shot through the building. Four shooters at once and water underfoot left no stance that worked; what would have served (out of their sight behind the bank) was the retreat, which found no route in the water.
368. *mid-202-i, "slain by Zombie" at minute 74, at a shelter being built at y 102.* Two zombies; the shelter chosen for the night was walked to with one hitting from behind, and at the site the emergency rule sealed the half-built walls, four seconds of building with a zombie one to two blocks off, from 12.7 to 7.2, because its "no sealing with a mob at arm's length" counted only mobs in sight and the zombie was round the wall. Then the fight, six critical swings in six seconds, too late. A biter within arm's length now rules out sealing whether it is in view or not. (The swings are in the record now as the defend frames' swing kind; the shield's being up is in the keys, note 365.)
369. *mid-242-l, "shot by Piglin" at minute 125, twenty-five minutes into its Nether (fortress reached at 105).* In iron with no gold worn, crossbow piglins went for it at y 42; the retreat found no way, and the rule's "hold defensive position" stood under their arrows forty seconds, from 6.7 to none. At the crossing Jev had chosen to cross without gold every time it was asked, and the gold option it was shown began "undefined Gold: no piece of golden armour carried": its first sentence, the one that says what the option does, was missing from the kit's texts. It now says: make golden boots first and wear them, four gold ingots, smelted from raw gold or gold ore. (Why no stance but holding was left at the end: the retreat and the fight had failed within twenty seconds; not changed.)
370. *mid-244-l, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 42, at y 36.* Stalking a skeleton at 6.7 health with a creeper, a zombie and two skeletons about, the step off an edge (a deadly drop near) chose the cell beside the feet, (49, 36, 120) from 48.8: the walk to it came back at once each time without moving, and the step was taken a hundred times a second until an arrow and the blast. Note 339 ruled out the cell stood on; this was the next cell over, and the walk went nowhere all the same. A step off the edge that does not move the bot now sets itself aside for five seconds, and the rest of the turn (the stance, the swing) answers.
371. *mid-229-h, "burned to death" at minute 143, twenty-one minutes into its Nether (reached at 122).* On a fortress leg the path ran along a one-wide netherrack ridge at y 35, the lava lake at 30 and 31 below on the one side; the bot walked it upright and slid off at z -81.5 with nothing pushing it, into the lava, out of it at 6.8 and burned from there to none. The edge's cost (note 217) still takes a ridge that is the way. While a walk is under way in the Nether, the bot now crouches on any cell beside a drop into lava or one that would take half its health or more, as a player does on a ridge (a crouching body cannot step off an edge), and lets go of the crouch before a step down the path means to take.
372. *mid-202-j, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 17, at full health.* Walling a pocket for the night, the build stopped for a creeper that would go off before it closed (note 292's check), and returned; the next tick began the same pocket and stopped again, a hundred times a second, nothing else answering the creeper, until it went off: twenty to none. The pocket given up for a creeper racing it is now set aside for ten seconds, and the shelter step does not begin a pocket so set aside, so the turn goes to the creeper.
373. *mid-207-h, a loop at minute 28: every way to lava resting.* The lava fetch for the cast had its pool and all four headings to the deep lava resting, and threw "every way to lava from here is resting" three passes running. Note 346 gave the ore descent eight headings at twenty-four and forty-eight blocks; the lava fetch and the obsidian step kept four. They now take the same sixteen, near first (tunneling.js descentTargets).
374. *mid-244-j, three hours and no Nether: thirty-eight minutes carrying lava a bucket at a time.* The cast frame at (48, 71, 129) was at the surface, the walk to the lava beside it given up (three walks came no nearer), and the lava sixty blocks down: with one bucket, ten trips. Making buckets was offered twice, as "twelve more from the 38 iron ingots, about 1 trip against 10", and Jev chose the cast as it was both times; by the later asks the iron had gone elsewhere. The offer now makes no more buckets than the lava still to fetch wants (nine here, not twelve), and says each trip's time to the nearest known lava and the minutes of walking either way. (Also: finding lava pools 15 minutes, food 14.)
375. *mid-244-m, "impaled by Drowned" at minute 38, at a river.* Climbing out to the surface at 8.9 health with a drowned throwing tridents: the shield came up to each one and went straight down, a hundred times a second, and two tridents took it from 8.9 to none. The guard rose for a shot within twenty blocks and held only while one was within twelve, so a trident twelve to twenty off raised and dropped it at once, blocking nothing. It now holds while the shot is still coming at the reach it rose for (to the hold's end).
376. *mid-244-o, "speared by Zombie" two minutes in, at y 14.* Spear zombies at 6.6 health: the pillar went up two, as told "zombies cannot reach a player two up", and a spear's thrust reached the bot on top three times, 6.6 to none. A spear reaches past an arm. The pillar now counts mobs with a spear among those that still reach it two up, in its cost, and says so: "A spear reaches past an arm: the zombie with a spear still reaches the bot two up."
377. *mid-227-k, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 75, at y -15.* Jev chose to leave a creeper be and work on (crafting an iron pickaxe); when it came within its fuse's reach and a threat again (note 329), the work stopped, and the stance, held fifteen seconds, ran again at every tick: "keep working", a hundred times a second, the creeper walking in. The dance was chosen only at three blocks, then a shot, and it went off, 10.4 to none. The same spin was in mid-202-h and mid-227-i. Leaving the mobs be is no longer held once one of them is a threat despite it: the stance is asked again at once.
378. *mid-244-n, "slain by Zombie" at minute 24, eating a golden apple that never went down.* With two zombies at arm's length at 9.6 health Jev chose the golden apple, and the stance "ate" it every half second for four seconds, none eaten, the zombies hitting throughout: the apple's run took the consume's return for a meal, and the consume came back at once. Mineflayer finishes an eat on any change of the held item, and the equip's own change, confirmed by the server a moment after the eat began, ended it. The eat stances now wait for the hand to settle before the bite, and the apple counts as eaten only when one is gone (a stance not done is set aside, as note 345 says). This is likely what cut the meals in notes 300 and 315 short too.
379. *mid-241-i, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 108, at 2.3 health and hunger nineteen.* Clear of the skeleton after a retreat, with health coming back on its own (hunger at eighteen or more), the survival question offered one thing, food (carrying on was off while a food plan was held); Jev chose the sheep fifty blocks off, told "a skeleton 9 blocks from the way, 34 along it" (note 352), and was shot on the way. Staying put while health comes back was not on offer. It is now: when health is under twenty and hunger is eighteen or more, "rest where it is" is offered with the health, the seconds to twenty and the mobs about, held half a minute (catalog updated).
380. *mid-229-i, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 91, twenty-four minutes into its Nether (reached at 67).* Beside lava at y 33 with a skeleton shooting, the run's first move, the step back from the lava edge, went nowhere each time (the walk came back at once) and was taken for done, twenty times a second; then the fight and the charge were chosen and it stood, shot four times more, 7.8 to none. A step back from the lava edge that does not move the bot now tries the next footing, and with none the rest of the run's answer goes on (note 370's rule, for the lava edge). Why the charge chosen did not move is not in the record.
381. *A guard for the spins (no trial of its own).* Notes 339, 370, 377 and 380 were one shape: a survival action that went nowhere (a walk that came back at once, a stance run again) reported itself as the answer twenty to a hundred times a second, the bot standing still, until a mob ended it. Each was fixed where it was found; the shape is now guarded in general: any survival action, an emergency's too, reported twenty times within a second with the bot not moving is refused for five seconds, said in the log, and the rest of the turn answers.
382. *mid-202-k, two deaths at minutes 90 and 91 (not fixed).* First, eight minutes into its Nether (reached at 86): walking a fortress leg along a ledge at y 89 over the lava sea (the route Jev chose), a blaze's fireball was seen eight blocks off and struck 1.3 seconds later, no shield raised in the record between, and threw the bot sixty-five blocks down into the lava. Why the guard did not raise the shield in the second and a third it had is not in the record (the fireball's speed may have carried it past the guard's look). Second, respawned without its armour, zombies at the surface took it from twenty to none in seven seconds, one swing landed. Neither is fixed; the first is watched for.
383. *mid-211-m, "slain by Enderman" at minute 118.* Going home for food at full health (just after resting to heal, note 379's option, chosen and used), an enderman was provoked; Jev chose to retreat three times, told only "a way is found, 2.1 seconds at a run", and each run ended with the enderman beside it again, 16 to none. The retreat now says, with an enderman among the mobs: an enderman after the bot teleports to it, a run from one ends with it beside the bot again (as it says of riders, note on mid-215-a).
384. *mid-235-k, a loop at minute 132, a fortress reached at 127.* With no building blocks left, every leg of the fortress search stopped at the first gap ("0 carried"), the crossing and the search trading names until the loop watch ended the trial. The block top-up was offered three times in the Nether and passed over, described as it is in the Overworld: "seal a pocket for the night or tower out of a hole". In the Nether it now says what the blocks are for there: every crossing over lava or a gap is laid a block a step and one with none stops at the first gap, and netherrack is all around and comes out in a moment with any pickaxe.
385. *mid-227-l, "fireballed by Blaze" at minute 47, at its fortress (reached at 44).* On a fortress bridge at y 77 under two blazes' fire: the span's rule held the bot still and crouched, five times in two seconds; the walls went up once (38:03), too late, and the shield came up only then, twice. The span rule's branch returned before the shield's turn in the survival tick, so with no walls to be had and no firm ground within reach, a shot on its way was simply stood in. The shield now comes up to a shot on its way there, crouched, before the hold.
386. *mid-243-k, three hours and no Nether (not fixed).* The run began underground with no pickaxe and climbed out eighty blocks by hand (the first half hour); then the cast frame went up at the surface at (74, 70, 80) and each lava trip went down and came back up the column: fetching lava 41 minutes, climbing back up 39, most of the day's 43 minutes standing still being the climbs a block at a time. Casting beside the lava was offered three times, with a trip's seconds either way, and Jev kept the frame at the surface; buckets were offered once. Judgment, with the facts given; nothing changed.
387. *mid-242-m, a loop at minute 127: its own portal boxed in.* The cast portal at (37, 72, 89) was lit, and on the way back to it the bot stood two blocks off: the cast's temporary walls (cobblestone and dirt) still stood in front of and behind the opening, only those inside the frame having been cleared, and the reservation round the frame made each of them "a building in the way", so no stair toward the portal was allowed and the walk found no way in. The doorway cells (in front of and behind the opening, the three above the floor) are no longer reserved, and once the portal is lit the cast's walls in the doorway come out.
388. *mid-211-n, "shot by Pillager" at minute 17 (not fixed; as note 367).* The same world's pillager patrol by the same stream: retreat, eat, pillar, seal and retreat chosen in turn while swimming, arrows every second or so, eighteen to none in twenty-five seconds. Two of the last three trials of first-days-211 ended at this patrol. Water under the feet undoes every stance that builds or runs; what would serve (out of the water, then out of their sight) is the thing to look at next if it comes a third time.
389. *mid-218-i, three hours and no Nether (not fixed).* No death or loop. The nights went on the night mine (41 minutes) and waiting in shelters (18); the days on gathering wool for a bed (20 minutes, sheep scarce), food (15), lava for the cast (17) and smelting. The cast frame went up late at (-675, 64, 572) after the walk to the known lava was given up 444 blocks off. A day spent on the bed that nights would have been slept through; nothing here stands out as one rule to change.
390. *mid-230-l, three hours and no Nether: an hour and forty minutes in a pocket with a creeper outside.* By day the pocket's question said "stay, though nothing is watching it" and Jev chose to leave sixty-seven times; each time every door was refused for a creeper drifting three to seven blocks off behind the rock, and the pocket waited (67 minutes) or swung at it through the wall (36). The watcher counted an unseen mob only within six blocks, and leaving shuts every door within six of a creeper up to sixteen off. A creeper within sixteen, seen or not, is now the pocket's watcher (so "open on it" and the rest are asked with it named), and leaving says what it does with a creeper by the doors.
391. *mid-242-n, "suffocated in a wall" at minute 17.* Tunnelling toward iron at y 35 under a column of gravel (y 36 to 37 and up), the staircase stepped into (36, 35, 76) and the gravel fell into the head cell as it did; it had already once dug out of gravel there a few seconds before. From 09:02 the bot took a point of suffocation every half second to none, and no dig out is in the record for those eleven seconds (the survival layer's last action was a meal begun at 08:58; what held the turn is not in the record). The staircase now never steps into a cell with sand or gravel standing over the head cell, level and down steps as the climbing ones already did.
392. *mid-227-m, "burned to a crisp while fighting Blaze" at minute 30, three minutes into its Nether (not fixed).* Stalking blazes at 11.9 health on a span at y 86, the bot held still on the span three times, a fireball struck (12 to 9.4), the walls went up and the shield came up (notes 332, 385), and the fire it had caught burned it from 6.4 to none over five seconds. In the Nether there is no water to put the fire out and nothing but waiting burns it off. The hunt at under twelve health with blazes about was the run's own step (the review's open item on the hunt's thresholds); watched.
393. *mid-231-k, three hours and no Nether (not fixed).* No death or loop. With no bed, the nights went on the night mine (65 minutes, 342 coal) and pockets (27); gathering wool for a bed took 17 minutes and ended with none carried; food 15. The cast was chosen only in the last hour, its walk to the lava at (117, 11, 141) under way at the end. Nights without a bed cost about a third of any run; a bed earlier is the lever, and Jev was offered it. 3,362 of the run's 4,050 questions were the night mine's next target.
394. *mid-205-m, three hours, Nether at 113 and no fortress (not fixed).* No death. Of its hour in the Nether, fifty-one minutes went on the fortress search's crossings: legs of ninety-odd blocks at y 96 to 104 straight through solid netherrack, two blocks dug a cell, about six seconds a cell, thirteen legs and nothing seen from inside the rock. A search that tunnels sees nothing and moves slowly; the search's legs keep the height they start at rather than seeking the open caverns where a fortress would be seen from afar. A matter for the search's design (legs through open air where there is any, and down toward the fortress heights), noted here for the next look at it.
395. *mid-227-n, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 17, at y 33 (not fixed).* A skeleton and a spider in a cave: Jev went pillar, charge, pillar, eat, seal, retreat, fight, come down, pillar, and then chose the pillar four times running as the skeleton shot it from 14.4 to none, the pillar told "shooters still can hit" with its cost. The record shows the shield up (note 365's key) at four of the last five hits, which landed all the same: facing matters to a shield, and on the pillar the bot faces the nearest mob each pass (note 336), which need not be the one shooting. Watched: if a hit lands through a raised shield again, the pillar's look should go to the shooter.
396. *mid-244-p, "fell from a high place" at minute 122.* Swimming for shore in a pool at y 49 on a hillside (after an earlier fall into it), the shore search took the nearest dry landing within sixty-four blocks, which was eighteen blocks below the water's surface, and the walk to it went off the pool's edge: 14.7 health to none. A shore landing is now within three blocks below the water's surface; one farther down is reached by a drop.
397. *mid-229-j, a loop at minute 86 at a river (not fixed; with the water problems under study).* Mining oak logs on a riverbank at y 58 to 63, the bot went in and out of the water: the climbs out were staircases refused for "lava or water underfoot", the air ran low, "work free" climbed it out and the mine took it back in, and the loop watch called the mine and the climb trading names. The return to its portal 190 blocks off could not be planned in time ("took too long to decide path"). Handed to the study of fights and work in water.
398. *mid-243-l, "tried to swim in lava to escape Ghast" at minute 75, five minutes into its Nether (reached at 70).* On a one-wide span at y 38 over the lava with a ghast shooting, the span's walls were "put up" eleven times in four seconds (three sides each time) and none ever stood: the placings reported as done without the block there, and each report ended the turn before the shield or the step off (notes 362, 385). A blocked fireball's push then threw it into the lava. A span wall now counts only when the block stands after it is placed; with none standing the turn goes on to the shield and the step off. (Whether the placings failed or the fireballs blew the walls down is not in the record.)
399. *mid-231-l, "slain by Zombie" at minute 41, at y -11 (a slip in note 372).* At 2.5 health with a zombie and a skeleton close, the shelter step "sealed" its pocket a hundred times a second: note 372 set the pocket aside after a creeper race, but its check came after the seal's report, so each tick reported the seal and gave up. The general guard (note 381) set it aside twice, five seconds each, and the rest of the turn did not save it. The check now comes before the report.
400. *mid-207-i, three hours and no Nether (not fixed; as notes 341, 374, 386).* The cast frame at the surface at (33, 70, 319), seventy minutes on the way into the Nether without finishing it: fetching lava 36 minutes, the pockets 26, the night mine 16, smelting 12. The same shape as the other cast runs of the day: a frame far above the lava, fed a bucket or few at a time. Jev was offered casting beside the lava and making buckets with each trip's cost said, and kept this frame each time it was asked (three times).
401. *mid-230-m, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 61.* By a deadly edge with a creeper as the thing pushing, the step off the edge walked for two seconds toward the nearest firm ground, which lay by the creeper; the walk stalled a few blocks on and the blast took it from twenty health to none. With a creeper within sixteen blocks the step now goes to firm ground away from it, and the walk is short (two and a half seconds, giving up after most of one without progress) so the turn comes back to the fight.
402. *Jev and time (the user: "Jev doesn't care about time").* Every choice was told that real minutes are what the player counts, and each option said its own minutes, but no question said the run's: mid-207-i was offered casting by the lava three times over seventy minutes on its surface frame and kept the frame each time, told only of the twenty minutes just gone (note 400). Every question about playing the game now carries `runClock`: minutes played toward the goal, when each milestone was reached, what the bot is on now, and where the minutes went, all told and in the last half hour (a survival action by name, work by its rung and step), with a sense of pace said as a fact: a practiced player from a settled start with iron reaches the Nether within the first hour and has the rods and pearls within the next two. Played time is counted from the trail's fifteen-second samples, a restart's gap at most half a minute.
403. *Testing the run clock (note 402) on mid-207-i's own question.* The portal-way question at 61 minutes on the surface frame, asked of Jev again as recorded: the frame five times in five (0.90 against 0.08 for casting beside the lava). Three things were wrong with what it was told. The portal-way question is a work question and work questions were given none of the time facts, the clock included; they are now. The frame said "10 of the ten to cast" with 3 standing; the blocks standing are now counted. Its trips to lava were said as a walk on the level, 66 seconds, to lava 136 blocks down; a lava far below is now said to be so, with each trip down and back up that far. And the way held said its minutes and its blocks but not the two together: it now says the pace, "3 obsidian in 61 minutes (about 20 a block), the 7 still to come would take about 142 minutes more." Asked again with all of it the frame fell to 0.55 against 0.32; given also a minutes figure for casting beside the lava (from the casting time measured here), it was even (0.46 against 0.45). Casting beside the lava does not yet say its minutes from a measure; that is next.
404. *The fortress search's legs, after note 394 (mid-205-m: fifty-one minutes tunnelling at y 96 to 104, nothing seen).* The leg kept the height it started at because the level crossing (nether-travel.js crossToward, surveyed at the feet's height) ran before the staircase and always made a little ground through the netherrack, so the sweep never turned or descended, and the heading was the compass's own. Each heading is now surveyed before a leg begins (surveyLeg: cells of open air, how many over a drop of four or more where a fortress is seen from afar, cells of rock at the six seconds a cell mid-205-m measured, what stops it), and the leg is Jev's (fortress_leg, src/decisions/travel.js): one option a heading, and from more than eight blocks off y 64 a staircase toward the fortress heights (corridors and bridges mostly between y 48 and 75 over the sea at 31), which runs the staircase alone until the bot is within the band. Without Jev the heading with the most open air, the compass's own at a tie. Test: mob-hunt.test.js, the next leg of the fortress search is Jev's.
405. *A held pillar's facing, after note 395 (mid-227-n, hits through a raised shield).* On its pillar the bot looked at the nearest mob each pass; a shield covers only the way the bot faces. The pillar hold now faces a shot on its way first, then the nearest shooter in sight, then the nearest mob (survival.js shieldFacing), and the record says which. For note 382 (mid-202-k's fireball seen eight blocks off, no shield in 1.3 seconds): the guard counted a shot whose velocity had not arrived only within six blocks; it now judges such a shot by where it has moved since the last look (projectile-guard.js incoming). Tests: survival.test.js, a held pillar faces the shooter; a shot whose velocity has not arrived.
406. *The hunt's start conditions, after note 392 (mid-227-m, blazes at 11.9 health).* canBegin refused a fight under fourteen health or hunger, without the kit, alight under ten, or hungry with nothing to eat, and said nothing: a hidden rule. Those are facts now (mob-hunt.js fitness, fitnessSays) on every option of the hunt_target question and in its state, with what the code once required and why it mattered (health comes back only at eighteen hunger; fire in the Nether burns off only by waiting); the footing (dry ground, not a span, air) stays the rule. Left open: with no blaze in view, prepareMobHunt still recovers on the same fitness (cover, food, health) without asking; a fight is only chosen where one is in view, so the judgment Jev makes is the one that mattered here. Test: mob-hunt.test.js, the hunt's health, hunger, food, fire and kit are facts.
407. *A creeper beside a pocket, after note 390 (mid-230-l, a hundred minutes in a pocket).* The door rule (no door within six blocks of a creeper) stays: it is physical. What was missing was any other way out. The pocket's question now offers tunnel_out (survival.js passageOut, tunnelOut): a passage one wide and two high dug through the far wall away from the creeper, level, each cell checked for lava or water behind it and a solid floor, until its end is ten or more blocks from the creeper and at least four cells long; said with the direction, the cells, the time, the clearance at its end, and that it stops, the bot still enclosed, if the creeper comes round toward its head. The pocket is forgotten as one left by a door. Test: survival.test.js, a creeper that keeps every door shut.
408. *Fights in water, after notes 367 and 388 (mid-211-l, mid-211-n: shot swimming a stream).* The stance to get out of the water (reachShore) ran the threat check at each step and threw at the first pillager in sight, so it ended at once every time it was chosen; and its landings had to be twenty blocks from every shooter (safeFromHostiles), which a stream with four pillagers on it never has. Chosen as a stance it now runs without the threat check (the stance is the answer to the threats) and swims first for the bank the shooters cannot see (a ray from each one's eyes to a player standing there), the option saying how far that bank is and whether the nearest bank of all is in their sight. Tests: shore.test.js, out of the water in a fight; survival.test.js, in water with a drowned (the pillager).
409. *Silences in the survival layer, after notes 358, 366 and 391.* Nothing found by reading that would hold the turn for seconds while hurt: the meal has a three-second bound, the dig out of a block runs first in maintainVitals, and the stances report as they run. So the record now says who has the turn: every layer marks it as it takes it (src/turn.js takeTurn: the survival step, a stance and the mobs it was chosen against, a question out to Jev by id, a meal, the dig out of a block, the hunt, the work's step), and every flight frame carries the holder, the phase and how long it has been held (recorder/observer.js turn). The next such stretch names what held it. Test: recorder.test.js, every frame says who has the turn.
410. *mid-218-j, "killed by Witch using magic" at minute 90, back in the Overworld on the way to its portal at night.* A witch four blocks off: Jev chose to fight (6.5 seconds, about 13 health, estimated), then ate, then chose the retreat, which found no way. The survival layer went on to the night's shelter, whose held answer was a shaft pocket, and dug straight down beside the witch for twelve seconds with the shaft open over it; its potions came in (harming through the armour, poison), twenty health to none. The shaft as the night's shelter already waited for a creeper that would reach it and a biter at arm's length; it now waits too for a shooter in sight and in its range (a witch ten blocks), and the encounter goes back to Jev's stance question. Jev's own dig_down, chosen with the shots of the dig counted, is not refused.
411. *mid-244-q, "fell from a high place" at minute 158.* Staircasing down from y 41 toward lava at y -56, the step chosen went two down onto a one-block stone lip at the edge of a ravine, the next cell over open for forty blocks to a mineshaft's rails. A skeleton sixteen blocks off stopped the step mid-drop, the body carried on past the lip, and the survival layer's step back came too late: forty-three blocks, twenty health to none. The staircase checked the landing's floor and not what lay beside it. A step now does not land beside a deadly drop (lava, or a fall costing half the health or more, as the Nether crouch counts it, note 371); the staircase takes another cell, or says the drop is what blocks the way.
412. *mid-230-n, "obliterated by a sonically-charged shriek" at minute 28, in a pocket at y -25.* Sealed in for the night beside its obsidian work, the bot was asked five times over two minutes what next, with a warden seven to eleven blocks off, told only that it was outside (heard, not seen), as a wall keeps a zombie out; it stayed each time. The warden's anger grew and its sonic boom came through the wall three times in eleven seconds, twenty health to none; asked between the second and third, it stayed again (0.74). Nothing it was told said a wall does not stop a warden, and the only other way was the door past it, priced as fighting it. The pocket's choices now say what a warden does (finds a player by vibration and by smell, angrier at each sniff; angry at one it cannot reach, a boom through blocks and armour, about ten a time, within fifteen blocks across and twenty up or down), whether it is within that reach, and the booms taken in the last minute (counted from the server's damage events); and a way away from it: a passage dug through the far wall to seventeen or more blocks across from it (the creeper's passage of note 407, generalized). Asked again as recorded: before any boom, stay 0.45 against the passage 0.40 (it was stay 0.75 against the door); after two booms, the passage six times in six (it was stay six in six).
413. *mid-207-j, "slain by Zombie" at minute 42, at y 17 beside a dungeon (partly fixed).* Fetching lava, the bot fought zombies for a minute six blocks from a zombie spawner it was never told of: each stance question said "a zombie, about 2.5 seconds", and eight zombies came in the minute, three at arm's length at the end. It swung about once a second (near the iron sword's own pace) and killed three, but the spawner made them faster; a bunker chosen at fifteen health was dug with three zombies at 0.6 blocks and never closed, and it ate and fought on to none. Every stance now says a spawner within sixteen blocks, how far, that it keeps making its mob while the bot stays within sixteen blocks, and that beyond them or with it broken no more come; the state carries `spawner`. Asked again as recorded with the sentence, the choices stood (fight at full health against one, 0.84; the bunker at fifteen, 0.77): both fair answers told the truth. Open: the bunker's dig against biters already at arm's length, which is what failed.
414. *How mid-230-n came to its warden (note 412).* Night-mining down toward a lava pool at y -13 for obsidian, it met a creeper at y -7 and retreated: the retreat's footing, the one further from every mob with a route, was twenty-five blocks east and ten down, in a patch of the deep dark (466 sculk, four sculk sensors at y -27 and -28, a shrieker that can summon at y -29, four blocks under where it stopped). No question had ever said sculk was there. It mined a deepslate diamond ore in sight and set about its obsidian at the lava there, digging and placing within a sensor's hearing, and the warden came up eight blocks off within a minute. Now every question about playing the game carries `sculk` when sensors or summoning shriekers are within sixteen blocks: how many and how far, whether the bot is within a sensor's hearing, what it hears (walking, landing, digging, placing, eating, a chest; crouched walking none), that a shriek is a warning and the fourth in about ten minutes calls a warden, and the warnings so far (each shriek's darkness, counted in session.js). The retreat's footings leave out those within a sensor's hearing when any others are left. Open: nothing yet offers to move the work at hand away from sculk, or to do it crouched.
415. *Work by sculk is Jev's (after note 414).* Told of sculk, the bot still had only the work at hand to do beside it. Between work steps in the Overworld, within a sensor's hearing or sixteen blocks of a shrieker that can call a warden, Jev is now asked `sculk_work` once per patch (held five minutes): carry on as now; carry on walking crouched (five minutes of the crouch held on every walk, the note 371 crouch now in the Overworld too, let go when the time is up; digging and placing still heard, said so); or take the work out of the sculk's reach, to the nearest standing place out of every sensor's hearing within thirty-two blocks, with the lava and remembered places within sixteen blocks of the patch passed over for thirty minutes (`goal.quietZones`, filtered in the lava surface search and the landmarks). Asked in mid-230-n's place (four sensors, the nearest three blocks off, a shrieker six): crouched four times in four (0.57), moving away 0.35, carrying on as it was 0.08.
416. *mid-244-s, "shot by Pillager" at minute 25.* On a two-block pillar eight blocks from a pillager, a skeleton and another pillager about, the bot was asked what to do eight times in a minute: each time the fight and the charge were offered as "about 6.2 seconds and 2.9 damage", though the same texts said the ground toward the pillager "stops after 0 blocks". It chose the fight, which ended at once with nothing to swing at, then the charge, which ended at once, then the pillar; twice. Arrows came every three seconds, 19.8 health to none. A charge whose ground carries no step toward the shooter is no longer offered; with only shooters about, none at reach and no step toward the nearest, the fight is priced as the shots taken standing (about fifteen seconds' worth) with no end, and the held pillar says the same. Asked again as recorded: seal six times in six (0.35; the pillar 0.2, the fight 0.13), where it had been the fight three in three.
417. *mid-218-k, "slain by Wolf" at minute 33.* Out for beef among four cows, with a wolf pack near them, the bot was bitten five times in eleven seconds (4 each), twenty health to 1.7 and then none, and was asked only which cow to hunt next: no wolf was ever a threat, so no stance was asked and no fight fought. danger.js said a neutral mob "the bot struck" had turned, but only endermen and piglins were ever counted. Now any mob that hurt the bot is a threat for thirty seconds, and wolves, bees and llamas turn as a pack (as piglins did) when one of them has; the fight estimate knows wolves, bees, llamas, iron golems, pandas and dolphins. Whether a swing at a cow struck a wolf first is not in the record.
418. *mid-227-o, "slain by Wither Skeleton" at minute 94, at its fortress (reached at 75).* Ten blocks over the fortress's bricks, the bot was asked the way in with "threatsInView: none"; six wither skeletons, a blaze and a magma cube were at the bricks, out of sight. It dug straight down, landed ten blocks lower beside the blaze with the skeletons coming, every stance priced at two hundred damage or more and no route out, and was dead in five seconds from twenty. Every way to a fortress now says the mobs within sixteen blocks of its bricks, seen or not (the bot knows them through a wall), and that a way arriving among them arrives in their fight; the state carries `atTheBricks`. Asked again as recorded with it: the staircase six times in six (0.49; the drop 0.27), where it had been the drop five in six.
419. *mid-229-k, a loop at minute 75 ("No way back to the overworld portal at 290, 68, -49: undefined, and the walk made no ground", three times).* Back in the Overworld for food, the bot spent twenty minutes on the shore of a lake eighty blocks across that lay between it and its portal, 120 blocks off: the walks toward the portal made no ground, the staircase rested for water underfoot, and the detours and shore climbs went round and round, an oak boat in its pack the whole time. The way back to an Overworld portal now offers the boat crossing (Jev's choice, with the swim it saves and the boat carried, as on the other walks) before the staircase; and the error says the staircase's own reason, looked up by the area it was set aside under (it was looked up by the portal's cell, and said "undefined").
420. *mid-242-o, "slain by Piglin" at minute 125, on a span over the lava sea (Nether at 96).* Crossing on a fortress leg with no blocks left for walls, a piglin came along the span. The span hold had been reported over and over for a minute with the bot crouched still, as it should be, and the stall watch took it for a stall ("Hold on span isn't getting me anywhere. Something else, then") and set it aside: from then every hold threw at its report, the survival layer did nothing, and the turn went to vitals throwing "Threat nearby" hundreds of times (the turn markers of note 409 showed it). The piglin hit four times in four seconds, no swing, no shield, 20 to 6.6, and the last hit put it into the lava. The span hold is now a hold the stall watch does not measure, as the pillar's is; and a hold refused for any reason no longer ends the span's answer: the swing at a mob in reach and the shield still come.
421. *mid-241-j, three hours and no Nether (not fixed; the time sinks of the open list).* Where the three hours went, from the flight record: the night mine 25 minutes, looking for food 19 and gathering and hunting it 9 more, lava 21 (the fill and its tunnel), the walks to landmarks 11, the way into the Nether 10, back to the portal 9, climbs to the surface 8, eating 8, pockets and waits 13. Food was the thread through it: the reserve stood at 0 to 4 points against 12 to 40 wanted, and "search for food" was answered 205 times, once every five seconds while it searched, with the options saying nothing of how long searching had gone or what it had found. The trial began before the run clock (note 402) and ran most of its hours without it. Left for the food and night work with the other time sinks.
422. *mid-243-m, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 81 (not fixed; a trace added).* At night in a pocket at y 84, 9.3 health, a spider and a skeleton outside, Jev chose to sleep in a nook (0.41, leave 0.34); the sleep was refused for the monsters near, and it chose the nook again. Two tenths of a second later the bot left the pocket ("Moving on."), which nothing Jev chose said, and was up among them: a charge at the spider, a pillar with the skeleton shooting (hit to 7.4 and 5.5), a fall off it, a retreat under fire, none. Which path in the code walked it out could not be found by reading (the nook's sleep, its wall put back, the pocket's own branch and the shortage of blocks each read as not the one; it carried 178 blocks). Every leave_shelter now says in the record which code called it (`via`), so the next one names itself.
423. *mid-202-l, a loop at minute 172 in the Nether (reached at 145): "No way back to the nether portal at -21, 89, 22: backed off to a landing with no step toward it there either", 68 times.* Out of blocks on the fortress search over the lava sea, it was offered "mine netherrack for building blocks" and chose it sixteen times in seven minutes: each round the mine step walked ten seconds toward a netherrack target and dug nothing, the upkeep question came again, and it chose the same. With no blocks, every crossing toward its portal stopped at the first gap. A round of gathering blocks that gains none now counts: the option says how many times it was chosen in the last ten minutes and how many blocks came of it, and two rounds with none rest it ten minutes. Open: why the mine step walked and dug no netherrack there (a target out of reach over the lava sea, or refused near lava by note 347's rule, is not in the record).
424. *mid-241-k, "slain by Zombie" at minute 8, in a cave at y -14 mining deepslate gold ore.* Six zombies about the cave: a retreat at twenty health, fights, then a pillar held three times told "About 0 damage ... Two up, none of them reaches it"; a zombie hit it on top all the same, and with six at the foot the fight went from 14.3 to none (no spawner; the retreat had no way). The pillar's "two up" was two up from the bot's own floor, and the cave's floor is not level: a mob standing within a block of the pillar's top, on a ledge or a slope within five blocks, is now said to reach it and counted in the pillar's price. Whether the hit came from such a ledge is not in the record (mob heights are not kept in the flight frames).
425. *mid-244-r, a loop at minute 114 ("flipping cast_portal <-> enter_nether"), no Nether.* Casting its frame at the surface, for the top row the bot made a stand, a block put down beside the slot four blocks up; no walk reached it (the route survey walks, it does not build), the next round found no stand left to make, and "Nowhere to stand to pour into the frame slot at (49, 78, 146)" came back five times, between detours, until the loop watch ended the trial. A stand no walk reaches is now built up to: the route and the walk are tried again allowing a pillar with the blocks carried, as a player pillars beside a frame for its top row, and the pathfinder's own settings are put back after.
426. *mid-235-l, three hours and no Nether (partly; the time sinks).* Its hours: the climbs 38 minutes (21 up to the surface on the work's ascents, 17 on the survival layer's returns), the nights 39 (night mine 24, waits in shelter 15), mining 10, landmarks 9, food 8, lava 11. At the 40-minute asking of its surface cast (note 403's facts all present, the lava 20 blocks off and 15 below) it kept the frame, 0.62 against casting by the lava 0.31: fair on the trips, but its pace said "2 obsidian in 40 minutes (about 20 a block), 160 more", and those 40 were the portal step's passes with all they needed on the way, mostly a climb for a stone pickaxe, not casting. The way held now says where its minutes went, by the step each pass was on ("ascend to surface 14, mine 11, fill bucket 9, cast: pour 6"), beside the pace. The climbs and the nights are left with the other time sinks.
427. *mid-242-p, "tried to swim in lava to escape Piglin" at minute 27, four minutes into its Nether.* A crossbow piglin shooting, Jev chose the retreat (0.5); it ran eighteen blocks to a footing beside a three-block drop to the lava, the next arrow's knockback put it over, and three seconds of lava took it from 14.8 to none. The retreat kept its footings off edges only with a hoglin about (whose toss throws a player); an arrow pushes too. With a shooter about, a footing within two blocks of a drop into lava or one deep enough to take half the health is now passed over.
428. *mid-211-o, three hours and no Nether: the nights (partly fixed).* Nights were sixty-two of its hundred and eighty minutes (night mine 37, waits in a pocket 25), a bed in its pack the whole run, and sleep offered once. The night is asked at dusk (time 9,500 to 10,300), before a bed can be slept in (12,542), so the answer is a pocket; in the pocket at bedtime the only way to sleep was a nook dug out of its wall, which a sealed pocket takes only when rock closes it all round, never so for a pocket built on open ground. Now at bedtime in a pocket with a bed carried and no nook, Jev is offered to open the pocket, put the bed down on level ground within four blocks and sleep, with the monsters within the vanilla sleep range said. Asked again at one of its bedtimes (13:11): with a monster by the bed spot, the night mine still (0.55 against 0.3); with none, the bed six times in six (about 0.5 against the mine's 0.33).
429. *The night's bed planned at dusk (after note 428).* The night is asked before a bed can be slept in; with a bed carried and no nook to be had, the shelter question now offers to seal a pocket now and at bedtime open it, put the bed down on level ground within four blocks and sleep, said with the seconds to bedtime and the monsters within the vanilla sleep range. Chosen, the plan is kept ten minutes and carried out at bedtime from the pocket without asking again, as the nook's plan is.
430. *mid-244-t, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 70, at y -29 (not fixed; with the Fable advice).* After "Sealing up my little hideout", a skeleton two to three blocks off shot the bot every three seconds for more than twenty, 9.6 health to none, and the record holds no survival frame at all: the turn was vitals/maintain throwing "Threat nearby" every few milliseconds, the survival action stale. The shape of mid-242-o's (note 420), a survival step that returns without acting while a mob hits the bot; the path is not yet found. Put to the Fable advisor with the other open questions.
431. *The survival silences found (notes 420, 430).* mid-244-t's bot log: "seal_shelter ran twenty times in a second without the bot moving: set aside five seconds", six times over. The seal reported each tick and placed nothing (a skeleton two blocks off, in or at its shell), the spin guard set it aside, and Survival.step turned the set-aside into "nothing to do": every set-aside anywhere in the step was returned as false. The turn then went to vitals throwing "Threat nearby" every few milliseconds, and the skeleton shot it from 9.6 to none in twenty seconds. mid-242-o's span hold (note 420) was the same through the same door. A set-aside now answers nothing only when nothing is about: with the bot hurt in the last four seconds or a mob in sight within eight (or within three unseen), the encounter's own question (flee: the stances) comes instead. Why the seal placed nothing with a skeleton at its shell is left for the Fable advice.
432. *mid-231-m, a loop at minute 134 ("flipping work_free <-> mine"; not fixed, with the Fable advice).* Stuck at y 50 under a frozen lake, packed ice and pockets of water over it at y 52 and 53, the staircase up toward (163, 82, 361) set aside for pacing, the unstuck move dig_north_feet chosen four times running, then place_south and dig_south_over, the climb out chosen again. The shape of mid-205-n's (stuck at y -10 under its frame, the same staircase set-aside and detours round it). Put to the Fable advisor as one question.
433. *mid-242-q, a loop at minute 32, a minute into its Nether ("No iron ore in the nether: it is only found in the overworld", three times).* The iron leggings' mining had stalled in the Overworld just before the crossing; Jev chose the Nether first, and went through. In the Nether the stall's answer ("differently", "persist") put the stalled step back in hand when it ended, the Overworld's "mine 5 iron ore", and the work tried it, failed, and was asked the stall again, round and round. A step for where the bot is not now fails as its own kind (WrongDimension): it is dropped with the stalled step it came back as, and the ladder plans again for where the bot is; nor is such a step put back in hand after a stall's answer.
434. *mid-235-m, "burned to death" at minute 44, at y -54.* Scooping lava for its cast with a creeper about, the bot went into the pool, came out at 6.6 health still alight onto a cell with lava about it (a span), a water bucket carried. The douse is the vitals step's, and the work loop gives vitals the turn only when the survival layer has nothing to do: the survival layer rebuilt the span's walls every tick for the creeper six blocks off, and the fire took it to none in ten seconds. Burning, out of the lava and not standing in fire, the survival step now pours the water (or walks into water near) before anything else.
435. *mid-227-p, "tried to swim in lava to escape Blaze" at minute 77, nine minutes into its Nether (not fixed; the architecture question).* Stalking blazes along a one-wide span at y 86, the bot took their fireballs from 20 health to 2 over forty seconds, the span's walls rebuilt and the hold held, and was never asked anything: on a span the survival layer's span branch owns the whole encounter (walls, the hold, a step off only for a creeper or a press at arm's length), and the stance question is not reached. A last fireball knocked it off at 4.2 and it fell fifty-six blocks into the lava sea. Left with the question the user asked (2026-09-27: why a survival layer at all): the span branch, the vitals step and the survival step each keep the turn by their own rules, and today's worst deaths were in the handoffs between them.
436. *mid-244-u, "blown up by Creeper" at minute 52, on a mineshaft span at y 1 (not fixed; the architecture question).* Looting a minecart, a creeper came: "creeper close in" ran twenty times over five seconds without the bot moving, Jev chose the retreat (0.56), and the run ended on a one-wide span, where the span branch held the bot still ("hold on span") until the creeper went off beside it, 16.2 to none. The span branch steps off away from a creeper only while its last step off has not failed in the last ten seconds, and otherwise holds. The same shape as note 435: on a span the encounter is the span branch's alone and Jev is not asked.
437. *mid-211-p, "hit the ground too hard" at minute 33 (not fixed).* On its way to the portal at y 29, a creeper went off beside the bot within two seconds of the last frame without one, 20 to 2.2 (the record shows no creeper close before the blast: round a corner or out of the look); a second went off four seconds later, 3.2 to 0.7, and the knock put it down a four-block drop. Nothing in the record says which rule failed; the creeper's approach is not in the frames (mob positions are not kept in them), which is a gap in the record itself.
438. *The mobs in the record (after notes 424 and 437).* Several deaths today could not say where the mob that hit the bot stood, or when it came: a zombie on a ledge by a pillar's top (424), a creeper round a corner (437). Every flight frame now carries `mobs`: up to eight threats within twenty-four blocks, each with its name, id, distance, position and whether it is in sight.
439. *mid-241-l, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 61 (not fixed; the architecture question).* Swimming up a water column at y -8 for air (oxygen 16), not on the ground, the survival layer ran the edge step (off_the_edge, for a zombie and a skeleton) and then the span branch (rail_span, three walls), both written for a bot standing on something; the bot came out of the water and fell thirteen blocks, 20 to 10, among a zombie, a spider and the skeleton, chose the water's way out and then the retreat, and was shot through a dig down, none. A branch acting outside the situation it was written for, as notes 435 and 436.
440. *The first Fable advice, acted on (the user's three-hourly check-in, 2026-09-27).* Four of its points held up in the code and the record and are in: a survival pass that returns with a threat at hand now has the encounter answered, whatever path it returned by (the general form of note 431); packed and blue ice are rock a tunnel goes through (plain ice melts to water and is not), which had every heading under mid-231-m's frozen lake refused; the nook's wall is put back from where the bot stands when the walk to its stand fails (mid-243-m's pocket was left open so); and the bow is priced as every other stance is (the seconds of drawing standing still, what reaches the bot meanwhile), with the guidance's old arena verdict against it ("the bow 16 to 17 with no kill") taken out. Its other points are open and larger: the work digging the current shelter's shell (mid-244-t's tunnel through its own pocket), the bunker dug with biters at arm's length and its price, beds as an option with their minutes a night, climbs that dig a new staircase up instead of taking the way down back, the food search that loses "carry on", gathering building blocks one block per round, and a return to a frame nearly straight up that the staircase reads as pacing.
441. *The block reserve in one choice (the Fable advice on note 423).* A mining round for anything but ore ends after one block, so a reserve of sixteen chosen once was sixteen rounds, each ending in a new upkeep question. Once chosen, the reserve is now gathered round after round until it is met or a round gains nothing.
442. *mid-244-w, "killed by Witch using magic" at minute 23.* Mining oak logs, the bot was poisoned by a witch eleven blocks off, chose the retreat three times (0.86, 0.61, 0.45) with the witch walking after it, the poison holding it at one health for half a minute, and a harming potion from 9.7 blocks ended it; the golden apple it carried was on offer each time (up to 0.33) and never eaten. Nothing said it was poisoned, that poison never takes the last health while a harming potion does, or that a witch follows a player it has seen. Every stance now says the bot's own effects that bear on a fight (poison, wither, slowness, weakness, regeneration, with the seconds left), in the options and as `effectsNow`, and the retreat says a witch walks after a player and throws within ten blocks. Asked again at 1.0 health: the golden apple six times in six (about 0.52 against the retreat's 0.35), where it had been the retreat three in three. (The frames' new `mobs`, note 438, showed the witch at every step.)
443. *mid-202-m, "fireballed by Blaze" at minute 89, in the Nether (reached at 48; not fixed, the architecture question).* Crossing on a span toward a fortress it had found (cross_level, 0.51), two blazes twenty-three blocks off shot: the span branch blocked the shots and railed the span while the fireballs' fire took 20 to 9 in fifteen seconds (in the Nether no water puts it out), then the way back to the portal, then at 2.5 the first question asked, every stance more than the bot had. The shape of note 435: on a span under fire the span branch owns the encounter and Jev is asked only when it is too late.
444. *A span's encounter is Jev's when its answers are not holding (notes 435, 436, 439, 443).* On a span the span branch's reflexes (walls, the hold, the shield, a step off for a creeper) were the whole answer, and four deaths in a day were theirs to the end. Hurt twice in six seconds on a span, with Jev reachable and no span being laid, the encounter now goes to the stance question like any other, the retreat off it among the options; the span branch still holds while its answers hold. A narrow step toward the single arbiter proposed to the user; not the arbiter itself.
445. *mid-211-q, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 27, at y 35.* A skeleton 0.2 to 2.3 blocks off (in the frames' `mobs`) shot the bot every three seconds for thirty seconds, 15.7 to none, the survival layer holding the turn and never swinging. The log: "defend failed and rests three minutes: Took to long to decide path to goal!". Survival.step's catch sets aside any failed action for three minutes; for the fight's own swing that meant no swing at a skeleton within reach for the rest of the bot's life. A hold that fails (defend, fight, the pillar held, a shot blocked, the pocket's wait ...) now rests ten seconds; other actions keep the three minutes.
446. *mid-205-o, a loop at minute 123 ("flipping fill_bucket <-> retreat_from_tunnel"; not fixed, with the stuck-underground family).* At y -10 fetching lava from y -54, every staircase heading "backed off to a landing with no step toward it there either", set aside by turns ((-329, -54, 521), then (-312, -54, 520)), the tunnel's retreat and the fill trading turns, navigation timing out in place between. The family of notes 432 and the mid-205-n loop: the Fable advice (a level passage toward open rock, then down; climbs and descents nearly straight up or down by the column rather than a staircase) is the next thing for it.
447. *mid-244-x, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 27, in a mineshaft at y 0 (not fixed).* A creeper, a skeleton and cave spiders; the charge at the skeleton (0.6), the creeper dance (0.89), then the fight (0.32) each ended at once (the skeleton three to six blocks off, the closing run not carried to it), and each stance that fails is left out for twenty seconds: with those gone the one stance left, digging down, was taken without asking, and the bot dug for twelve seconds under the skeleton's arrows, 8.6 to none. Two things: the closing run on a skeleton a few blocks off failing where a player walks up and strikes it, and the lone stance left after the failures being run unasked when it is one Jev had put last. The frames' `mobs` show the skeleton at 3 to 6 blocks throughout.
448. *The fight at a shooter the charge will not run at (after note 447).* The fight closes on a mob by the charge, and the charge refuses more than eight blocks, more than two up or down, a bot beside a drop, or lava by the target; against a shooter it would not run at, the fight ended at once, though it was offered as a kill. With the nearest a shooter out of reach, nothing at arm's length, and the charge refused by those rules, the fight is now said and priced as standing in its fire (note 416's case, widened from "only shooters, no step" to this).
449. *mid-230-o, three hours and no Nether (the time sinks; its own run clock).* At minute 127 its run clock said: night mine 29, obsidian 13 and lava 10 for a cast chosen at 15:26, eating 7, the way in 7, landmarks 7, wool for a bed 7, food 6, and at the end back on a stone pickaxe. No death; the nights and the obsidian again, as in notes 421, 426 and 428.
450. *The bed weighed with the night's minutes, and a stale search (the Fable advice; notes 428, 449).* Where the bed is weighed (its rung, and "Nether first" leaving it) it now says what a night costs without one: about eight and a half real minutes from bedtime, eleven from dusk, three nights to an hour of play. And the bed's rung said "Searching for sheep for 1033 minutes ... none seen yet" to a run an hour old: the wool search carried from the first days' world was never restarted. A search left off over half an hour ago is now a new one, and not said. Asked again (mid-244-y's first ladder question, recorded bed 0.47 against Nether first 0.44): with the run clock and the night's figures, Nether first 0.7 against the bed 0.25, three in three; the stale search was still in that replay's text.
451. *mid-227-q, "fireballed by Blaze" at minute 80, four into its Nether; cover from shooters (with notes 435, 443).* On the fortress search at y 72 a blaze twenty blocks off burned the bot down from 6.6 (the fireballs' fire, which no water puts out in the Nether), and at 1.4 it was offered the pillar, the seal, the retreat, the fight: nothing that breaks a shooter's line. Every stance with shooters in sight now offers cover: a block two high in the line of each (up to three), beside the bot, with its blocks, seconds and the fifteen seconds' damage priced as the others are (only what does not shoot still reaching), run by the wall-off the trapped bot already had, now at any distance; the shield comes up behind it if carried. Asked again as recorded (1.4 health): cover six times in six (about 0.87), where it had been the pillar.
452. *mid-207-k, three hours and no Nether: the climbs (the Fable advice).* Its run clock at 177 minutes: return to the surface 26, the ascents for a stone pickaxe 18 (forty-four minutes climbing), night mine 20, lava 20, waits in a pocket 11; the surface cast frame kept four times (0.66, 0.66, 0.55, 0.64). The way back up looked for surface within forty-eight blocks by any route that digs nothing, and failing that dug a new staircase: the stairs it had come down by, often further than that, were never walked. Now where a way down begins at the surface it is remembered (the last eight, by dimension), and a climb first walks back up to the nearest such top within 160 blocks by a route that digs nothing, before any new way is dug.
453. *mid-242-r, a loop at minute 92 ("No measurable progress on cast_portal ... stop_water", four times).* A frame slot held flowing water fed by a source at (38, 76, 89), walled in by the cast's own temporary cobblestone; the cast scooped at it round after round and the bucket never reached it, the night's pocket and its night mine (chosen eight times in thirty seconds) between. A feeding source is now filled with a block, tracked with the cast's other temporary blocks, when the scoop failed there once or no empty bucket is carried (with none it had waited to drain for ever); a scoop that brings no water back counts as failed.
454. *mid-211-r, a loop at minute 59 ("flipping tunnel <-> enter_nether"; not fixed, for the Fable advice).* Fetching lava at y 33 from a pool at (136, 33, -185) eight blocks off at its own level, the bot paced cells at (126..129, 33, -178..-189) for more than a minute, "the staircase toward (136, 33, -185) is not gaining on it; starting round 2": the rule that refuses a dig within three blocks of lava (note 347) refuses every cell a tunnel at the lava's level would open to reach it. How a pool at the bot's level is to be reached (from above, scooping down into it; or with the lava's own edge walled first) is put to the next advice.
455. *mid-205-p, "slain by Zombie" at minute 64, at y 2 at night (partly fixed).* Among three zombies, a spider, a skeleton and two creepers, the stance changed every one to three seconds: fight, dig down, the pillar, eat, dig down, the seal, and none was carried through; digging down was chosen three times with a biter within three blocks, and its shaft stops for one at once (note 410's rule), so each ended as it began and the crowd had the bot between. Digging down is no longer offered while a biter is within three blocks (the older crowd test's spider moved from 1.8 to 4.2 so it still prices the dig). The seal's own failures ("45 seconds on seal failed without getting anywhere", "no route") and the stance-by-stance churn in a crowd are left with the architecture question.
456. *mid-229-l, "slain by Zombie" at minute 176 (not fixed).* At 1.3 health at y -4 with two zombies and a spider at arm's length, the edge step ("off the edge") was reported some twenty times in a second without the bot moving before the fight was taken, and a hit ended it. Lost before then; the churn is the crowd shape of note 455.
457. *mid-230-p, "shot by Skeleton" at minute 28.* A skeleton seven to nine blocks off (seen, in the frames' `mobs`) shot the bot every three to six seconds for thirty seconds, 9.7 to 0.4, while the work spun on its climb ("ascend to surface" some two hundred and fifty times a second) and the survival layer answered nothing; the stance question came at 0.4 (cover, 0.85) and the next arrow ended it. A mob a charge could not reach is left out of the threats for twenty seconds unless a hit landed in the last four, on the reading that it "cannot reach the bot either": true of a walker on a ledge, not of a shooter, whose arrows came further apart than those four seconds. A shooter is no longer left out so.
458. *mid-218-l, three hours and no fortress (Nether at 120): a loop the note 433 fix made.* Its last hour in the Nether was "No oak log in the nether: it is only found in the overworld" some thirty-seven hundred times in ten minutes, standing still, the run clock calling it "between steps" (55 minutes of the run). Note 433 drops a step for another dimension and lets the ladder plan again; here the plan came back to the same oak logs every pass (the record does not say from which step: the held iron pickaxe rung, or wood its tools want). Now the same wrong-dimension step three times in a minute sets aside the ladder stage it came from for ten minutes and lets go of the held strategy choice, each pass waits a second, and the record keeps where the step was asked from (`goal.wrongDimension.from`). Open: planks and sticks in the Nether from crimson and warped stems rather than oak.
459. *mid-202-n, "fell from a high place" at minute 81, from full health.* Swimming in a flooded column with a creeper near, the survival layer ran the edge step and then the span branch's step off, both written for a bot standing on something; they walked it out of the water and it fell thirty blocks, 20 to none. As note 439 (thirteen blocks, mid-241-l). A swimmer is now on no span (the water holds it), and neither edge step runs while the bot is in water.
460. *"None of these options are good" (the user's suggestion, 2026-09-27).* Many of the day's fixes were a move a player would have made that was not on the list (cover from a shooter, a passage away from a warden, the bed beside the pocket, the golden apple). Every question about playing the game now also offers Jev "none of these options are good: the move a player would make here is not among them", to be chosen only when such a move is missing and not because every option is costly. Chosen, it is recorded in artifacts/missing-options.jsonl (the question, the state, every option's text, Jev's weights, where and when), marked `noneGood` in the goal's decisions, and the best listed option by Jev's own weights is taken all the same, so it never stalls. The test runner turns it off (JEV_NONE_GOOD=0), since its tests name each question's options; its own test turns it on.
461. *Jev's close calls (the user's suggestion, 2026-09-27; scripts/low-confidence.js).* A report over the flight records: gameplay decisions whose top weight was under 0.45, by question, and those a death followed within a minute with what was on offer. Today from 11:00: the encounter stance 2565 asked, 252 close, 37 of them within a minute of a death; every other question together 7 (the stall's detour 3, the pocket 2, upkeep 1, the fortress approach 1). Among the 37 the pillar was the pick in nine, most at low health with shooters or blazes about. With "none of these" (note 460) it is gone over at each advice check-in.
462. *"None of these" tried on the day's cases (the user asked, 2026-09-27).* Recorded questions asked again with the new option, where the move was missing and where it was there: mid-230-n's warden booming through the pocket, no way away offered, flagged four in four; the same with the passage away offered, the passage two and a flag two; mid-227-q's blaze at 1.4, cover missing, not flagged (the pillar 0.37, "none of these" 0.32, a close second each time); the same with cover offered, cover four in four; one zombie at full health, the fight four in four; mid-205-p's crowd at 1.0 with every real option there, the seal three in three ("none of these" 0.15). So it does not hedge a lost cause, and it can miss by a little: a "none of these" weighed a quarter or more and not taken is now recorded too, as a near flag.
463. *mid-242-s, "burned to death" at minute 29, two into its Nether.* A ghast's fireballs and the fire they lit took it from 20 to none in sixteen seconds while the work churned on "No iron ore in the nether" (the loop of note 458, not yet deployed to this bot). The ghast was never in the frames' `mobs`, which reached twenty-four blocks; shooters are now recorded out to forty-eight. Fire from a ghast in the Nether, with no water to put it out and cover the only answer (note 451), is the rest of it.
464. *Faster (the user asked, 2026-09-27).* Two tools. Stage snapshots: checkpoint.sh keeps a snapshot the first time each trial stands in the Nether or at a fortress (scripts/trials/milestone.js reads it from the flight record), and scripts/trials/start-stage.sh starts a trial from one, the least used first; mid-227-r-nether-1 was the first, in the Nether from its first minute. The replay suite: evals/replays/cases.jsonl holds ten recorded decisions from the day's deaths, their text as the fixes left it, each with the answers expected and those never to win, and scripts/replay-suite.js asks Jev each again through decide() (seconds a case, no Minecraft). First run: nine pass, one a known gap (the pillar taken against a blaze at 1.4 health though its text says two up does not stop one).
465. *mid-231-o, a creeper at 0.9 health at minute 14, hunting food for the Nether.* Seven minutes under two health with no food; the shelter search timed out and was read as "unreachable", and the turn fell through to the work. A creeper came from eight blocks to three with no stance asked. One cause was plain: every food step (cook, craft a sword, hunt, search) and survival's two walks set the task's threat check for themselves and cleared it to nothing when done, so the step that had called them ran on unwatched. Each now gives the check back as it found it. The fall-through to the work and the timeout read as a fact are the rest (the single arbiter's work, and the next note).
466. *Four deaths at once, triaged side by side (the timeline script and four hands).* **mid-231-o** (note 465): its shelter search had four hundred milliseconds under the day's load, ran out, and "timeout" was read as no way there and set the shelter aside ten minutes; a search out of time now looks longer once, and out of time again rests a minute, not ten. **mid-230-q**, "shot by Skeleton": knocked into the air at a ravine's rim by a zombie, the edge rule measured and planned from the cell over the drop, and its route's first move was a pillar there; it fell fourteen blocks, 15.1 to 4.1, and a skeleton finished it. The edge step now waits to land, plans from where it stands, and its walk does not tower in place. **mid-207-l**, "slain by Spider": at 5.2 health with hunger 16 (no healing) and no food, underground by day, it chose the food search, told nothing but "walk to another dry area", climbed out through its cave, and a zombie and a spider met it. The search now says its climb to the surface first, the health, that it does not come back, and the mobs within twenty-four. Underground at night with health not coming back, sealing in until dawn is offered beside the climb to food (the night is otherwise no question down there), and the food options say the surface is night.
467. *mid-229-m, a loop "flipping persist <-> fill_bucket" at minute 50.* Beside the only lava pool it knew, every staircase into it resting, the fill step "arrived" and did nothing, about twenty-five times a second, and persist put it back each time. The staircases rest keyed on the block above the lava; the pool filter asked after the lava's own block, in another cell, and passed the pool as open. One check now (tunneling.js lavaResting, on the block above), used by the pool filter, the dig target and the lava option; a step at a pool it cannot work goes on to the other ways, and with every way resting says so as a fact ("the pool at (342, 47, 33) is the only lava known; every way into it rests for 10 more minutes ...") that goes to Jev once a round, not twenty-five times a second.
468. *mid-227-r, "doomed to fall" at minute 55, in the Nether.* On a one-wide basalt bridge it had laid at y 87 over a forty-four-block drop, two blocks left, it chose to gather blocks, told "netherrack is all around and comes out in a moment"; the nearest it would dig was thirty-three blocks off and fifteen up across open drop. A blaze twenty-seven off fired for thirty seconds, each shot cutting the mine step short, and a round cut short was never counted, so the reserve question came back every eight seconds with no "none gained" to say; a fireball's knockback put it over. Now: a round cut short is recorded with what it gained, so two empty ones rest the gather as any two do; the block_reserve option in the Nether says the nearest block it would go for, how far up or down and how many columns of open drop lie on the way; and the risk the bot is told of counts shooters out to forty-eight blocks and shots in the air coming at it, where it said "nothing hostile in view". That the span itself under fire is a place to leave, not a place to parry, is still open (the arbiter's).
469. *mid-211-s, "tried to swim in lava" at minute 79, seven into its Nether.* On a span at y 89 over the lava sea it fought a magma cube, told the drop a block off and that a knock over it was the end, beside a fight of 2.2 damage; no way to fight anywhere else was offered, and the second hit threw it forty blocks down into the lava. A player steps back onto firm ground first: fight_from_footing is now a stance where the drop beside the bot is lava or half its health and ground three blocks from any drop is within eight, said with its distance and seconds (the walk does not tower in place). And the fight there says what its damage hides: how many of the mobs' hits land by the estimate, each a knock that can put it over. The recorded decision, replayed: fight five in five with the new stance alone, the new stance five in five with the hits said too. The replay suite now asks each case five times, not three: at three, one stray answer failed a case.
470. *mid-211-s-nether-1, the stage snapshot of note 469 started again, dead in ten seconds.* The snapshot the checkpoint kept was the span with the magma cube beside it, so the death came again at once, on the new code: fight chosen, the hits said, and no ground to fight from, since the search for it reached eight blocks and the span ran further. It reaches sixteen now, the walk's time said and its timeout scaled to it. A stage snapshot taken at a bad moment is a repeatable test of that moment: started again after this, as one. The timeline script picks its flight files by the moment asked about (a new trial on the port hid the old one's).
471. *mid-211-s-nether-2, the snapshot of note 469 a third time, dead again in eleven seconds.* The ground search of note 470 found nothing within sixteen: read off the snapshot's region files, the bot stands on a one-block ledge along a netherrack wall with lava running through it, nothing three from a drop anywhere near. A player there walls the open edge at the feet and fights: the span's own railing (railSpan), which survival raised only as a reflex, is now a stance Jev can choose, rail_and_fight, said with the sides, blocks and seconds, the fight after it. The snapshot runs again as its test.
