Tests (turn-handback.test.js, from the recorded states): after the meal the piglin two below, set aside by the charge, is survival's claim and turn_priority is asked, the spear's way up judged by walk-reach; the work holding the turn, the piglin come to 1.3 across and 1.6 below preempts it by reach, not while four off, and a held ruling for the work is asked again; the spear's reach by the jar's geometry (the diagonal at 3.5, not 2.9 straight ahead, not from below the feet, not a crossbow); the crossbow piglin come from 28 blocks to 6 by the nineteen-block drop preempts the work's 57-second turn by push, survival's claim saying the drop, a held ruling asked again; the ghast at 41 in sight left be by Jev waits out his fifteen seconds, then preempts; a turn given with the push is not stopped for it again. The spear fight at the lava edge offers shield_the_charge, says 6.7 a blow, and every open stance says the knock over the drop; the fight's hold and the shield stance raise the shield. danger.test.js and combat-estimate.test.js: the charge's set-aside now shown with a climber, a walker on open ground a threat whatever the charge found. Each fails without the change. npm test 1887 of 1887, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 48 of 49 with the known gap. Still open: the kinetic charge's own damage by speed (mid-87-m's thirteen) is not modeled beside the piglin's eight; whether the bot's shield holds a spear's charge is the game's rules only until a trial shows it; the arm's-length reflex at six health and under is unchanged beside the new reach rule; pushersAbout counts a blaze in sight to 48 by a deadly drop, so fortress bridges will ask turn_priority more (once per ruling), not yet seen live; none of this has run live.
587. *Three hoglin deaths: mid-208-k-nether-1 (25587, bitten to death at 01:30:01 while it ate at 4 health), mid-208-k-nether-4-fortress-1 (25589, a minute between hoglins at 1.6 to 2.8 health, fight and eat in turn, "shot by Piglin" at 04:46:00) and mid-244-ab-nether-3 (25586, 05:46:56, two hoglin blows from 5.1 after a pillar that put no block down).* Read from the 26.1.2 jar: a hoglin's blow is half its six and up to five more (HoglinBase.hurtAndThrowTarget), three to eight before armour, 5.5 on the average; it strikes every forty ticks (HoglinAi MeleeAttack.create(40); a baby every fifteen for half a point, and it throws nothing), where the other biters strike every twenty; its 0.6 knockback resistance keeps a sword's knockback from putting it back; each blow that lands throws the target on top of the usual knockback (0.2 to 0.7 of a block a tick back, up to half a block a tick up); it chases at 0.3, 3.9 blocks a second, as the table had; a zoglin is the same but shuns nothing. HoglinSpecificSensor looks for a block in hoglin_repellents (warped fungus, potted warped fungus, a nether portal's block, a respawn anchor) within 8 across and 4 up or down of the hoglin's own cell, about once a second, and BecomePassiveIfMemoryPresent, first in its idle and its fight activities, then drops its target and pacifies it for 200 ticks, again as soon as that ends while the block is that near; idle it walks off to 8 from the block; struck, it turns on the striker (wasHurtBy erases the pacifying) and is calmed again the next tick near the block. Only a block counts: a warped fungus held or carried does nothing. It stands only on supports_warped_fungus (nylium, soul soil, mycelium, dirt, coarse and rooted dirt, grass, podzol, moss, mud, farmland), not on netherrack, soul sand, blackstone or basalt. Measured in the flight records to 2026-09-28: 37 hoglin blows through iron came to 1.4 to 4.8 (3 and 8 before armour through fourteen points are 1.5 and 4.8), the body carried up to 4.2 blocks back and 2.9 up within a second and a half (1.3 back on the median, walls and ceilings stopping many), and mid-208-k-nether-1's hoglin struck at 01:29:28.6, 30.6, 32.8, 34.9 and 36.9 while it was being struck. What the questions said: every stance opened "hits for about 3.4 a blow through the armour worn (6 before it), a blow a second at arm's length: 5 blows end the bot from 13.9 health", where the hardest is 4.8 and three did (04:45:11 to 04:45:14.6, 13.9 to 1.6); the fight priced the hoglin being struck at a third of a blow a second and the others at one; the meal at 04:45:43, with the hoglin at the bot 0.9 seconds into its 1.6, "About 2.3 damage ... while it eats", 0.7 seconds of a steady rate where the first blow lands whole; the toss was said as "about three blocks" and the drop was looked for within three; and warped fungus, which the bots pick up in the forests (52 flight records carry it), was never offered. Now (combat-estimate MOBS hoglin, zoglin: hit 5.5, least 3, most 8, blowEvery 2, struck 0.5, toss 4): the fight prices a hoglin at a blow every two seconds, struck or not; every stance's price (stanceCost) lands a biter's first blow whole the moment it arrives during the stance, then its pace (a biter at arm's length already keeps its steady rate: where its next blow falls is not known), a blow counted whole under the half second a hurt body cannot be hurt again (within, blowAt); every stance opens "hits for 1.5 to 4.8 a blow through the armour worn, about 3 on the average (3 to 8 before it), a blow every 2 seconds at arm's length: 3 blows at their hardest end the bot from 13.9 health (5 on the average)" (survival.js blowsSay), and the hits left say the hardest ("about 3 each after armour, up to 4.8: 3 at the hardest"); the meal's claim carries the hardest blow and the pace in its facts (vitals.js comingWhileEating hitsForAtMost, blowEverySeconds); with a hoglin or zoglin that can get to the bot the drop is looked for within four and said with the toss ("A hoglin's blow throws the bot up and back, up to about 4 blocks back and three up, not a step"). A new stance, warped_fungus (src/hoglin-repellent.js, survival.js warpedFungusOption): offered where a hoglin can get to the bot, a warped fungus is carried, and an open cell within reach stands on ground that takes it, or on a floor to lay such ground carried first (dirt, nylium and the rest: two blocks); said with the rule above (only the block counts, a portal and an anchor do the same, nothing else is kept off, the grounds it takes and does not), where it goes, the seconds placing, the second before the hoglins notice, and the price: every mob hitting while it goes down and that second, then all but the hoglins; held, with it standing, it is staying by it. The danger list leaves out a hoglin with such a block within its box (danger.js hostileEntities, hoglin-repellent pacified), so a meal or the work goes on beside the fungus or a portal, and the portal_back option says a hoglin shuns a portal's blocks. mid-244-ab-nether-3: at 05:46:53.2 Jev took the pillar at 0.87 (hoglins at 7.1 and 11.6, 5.1 health); pillarFrom returned in six milliseconds with "0 of 2 blocks went down", no jump made: pillarUp stops before any wait for lava or water in or beside the cells the body rises through, or fire, a cobweb or a block it does not dig over the head, while the offer looked only at three cells of air over the feet; the next question (05:46:53.8, the hoglin at 4.1, then 2.7) spread its answer and took the fight at 0.28, and two blows ended it. The terrain is not in the record, so which stop it was is not known. Now the climb's stops are one test (pillar-recovery climbStop), used by pillarUp and by the stance for both of its blocks, and a pillar that stops says where ("0 of 2 blocks went down: the climb stops at lava at ..."). At the same moment the arbiter's held ruling broke on "a newcomer within six blocks": the hoglin the survival claim was already answering, 7.1 blocks off at the ruling and 4.1 at the break, counted as new (arbiter.js broken compares the ids within six at the ruling); it cost a turn_priority asking of 0.2 seconds, not the pillar, and is left to the arbiter's own work (note 586). Probes (5 asks each through decide): mid-208-k-nether-4-fortress-1's 04:45:06 question as recorded, fight 5 of 5; with the texts as fixed (the fight 61.1 where it read 93.5, the blows from least to hardest), fight 5 of 5 (retreat said no way out, no other stance offered); with a warped fungus on the nylium added (none was carried; 6.1 damage, "By the fungus, none of them reaches it"), warped_fungus 5 of 5 (replay case hoglins-three-warped-fungus-offered-208k). Tests (test/hoglin-fight.test.js, each failing on the old code): the jar's blow, pace and the struck rate; the stance opening for 04:45:06; the meal's first blow whole at 04:45:43; a hoglin within the fungus's or a portal's box left out of the danger list, the item carried not, one five up not, a zoglin not; the fungus offered on nylium with its words and price, run and held, not on bare netherrack, on laid dirt with it carried, not against a zoglin; the drop within the toss said at four; the pillar not offered where its climb stops, and its failure said. combat-estimate.test.js: the second cave spider's first bite lands 0.13 seconds after the first's and is mostly lost under the half second. npm test 1886 of 1886, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 47 of 48 with the known gap (blaze-at-1.4-cover-missing). Still open: a player fights a hoglin shield up and strikes between its blows (the swing reflex raises the shield after each swing but does not time the swing to the hoglin's two seconds); the fungus is left where it is set, and a player would pick it up on leaving; the fight's closing (PACE.leadBites) is not scaled to a hoglin's pace; none of this has run live.
589. *mid-242-ab-nether-3-fortress-1 (25592), 58 minutes at its fortress, "wait in shelter" 25 of the last 29, no new ground: the audit's turn_priority 26 times the same answer was survival, and the survival was pocket_next, stay every time for 33 minutes.* Not a new wait: the same sealed pocket as note 584, its question now said with 584's facts, and still stay. What it was: at 05:06:49 the bot ate its last rotten flesh; at 05:07:05 Jev chose leave_nether go_back (0.49, for food, nothing carried); on the way to the portal (return_to_portal, then cross_toward "the portal back", 280 blocks off) a blaze 7 blocks off had encounter_stance take_cover and then seal (0.51), and at 05:09:08 it was sealed in at (-180, 58, -191), 15.8 health, hunger 16. It stayed until 05:42:17, pocket_next stay 0.43 to 0.64 at every asking (none_good 0.16 to 0.33), turn_priority survival between. It left (0.70) only once nothing was within sixteen blocks, the skeleton 25 off. What it waited for: nothing that would come. No daylight in the Nether (584 said so), the blazes keeping about their fortress (585 said so), and health, which could not come back: hunger 16 with nothing to eat, standing still. That last was said as "not healing ... health comes back only at eighteen or more", never that nothing carried could get it there. Most askings had nothing within sixteen, two or three blazes 22 to 41 off, and stay still won. What the options said wrong or left out. The leave said "go back to the obtain blaze rods step" (workWaiting reads the rung) when the work was the trip back for food Jev had chosen two minutes before the seal, which went on as return_to_portal the moment it left. No way to food was offered: go_for_food's ways were surveyed only in the Overworld, and the pocket's tree left out return_for_food and hoglin_food. The bot was restarted at 05:21:58 (571 to 585 going live), and 584's record began at the first look after it: "13 minutes so far" at 05:34 for a pocket sealed at 05:09, and the blaze it was sealed against was forgotten (the record looks for the sealing stance within two minutes of its own start). The two blazes that drifted in were said as "come from 23 to 8 blocks off". They had been 4 to 24 and back, never with the bot in sight. Because they were nearer, 584's rule priced the leave "Out among them, fighting them all ... 27.5 damage (more than the bot has)". Now (pocket-wait.js): the wait runs from the seal. The seal pass already keeps where and when it sealed (state.sealing), and every step out of a pocket now keeps when the bot was last seen out (pocketOutAt). A pocket sealed since the bot was last seen out is one wait, across a restart or a gap in the looks, and a record begun after its seal is moved back to it. A new wait begins when the bot has been out or the pocket is sealed again (this replaces the half-minute gap rule). The sealing stance is read against the seal's time, and a stance record with its kinds as a string (from before 584) is read too. A mob come nearer is said with how near and far it has been over the wait ("come from 23 to 9 blocks off, from 4 to 24 over it") and, either way, whether it has had the bot in sight. Held off, for the price "should they all come at the bot at once", is now every mob outside about a minute or more with none having had the bot in sight in the wait and none the mob it was sealed against. Come nearer or not, it says "a mob takes the bot as its target only once it has seen it" (a warden aside). Hurt under eighteen hunger with nothing carried to eat (not even the last resort), the stay says "Nothing carried is food: hunger 16 does not rise in here, so health does not come back in this pocket however long it waits". Where no daylight comes either: "Neither daylight nor health comes to this wait: only the mobs outside moving off would change it" (state pocketSoFar.health). In survival.js, off the Overworld with Jev's trip back for food still held (netherLeaveHeld, ten minutes), the leave says it goes on with that trip ("as Jev chose at 05:07, before this pocket was sealed on it"), with portalTrip's distance, lava and health on the way, and says the rung waits till the bot is fed and back. go_for_food is offered off the Overworld too. Its ways are the food question's own offWorldFood: back through the portal, said with the trip, left out when the leave already goes on with it, and the Nether's hoglins when known. Stay and leave stay Jev's. Probed against the live model on the recorded askings, rebuilt through the changed code (survival.step with the trial's saved survival state and goal, the blazes at their recorded distances, the recorded dig_in_and_fight and state beside), 5 runs each. 05:13:05 (4 minutes in, the trip held, blazes 27 and 28 off): recorded stay 0.53, now leave 5 of 5 (0.54 to 0.61). 05:22:14 (13 minutes, blazes 35 to 41): recorded stay 0.55, now go_for_food/return_for_food 4 and leave 1 (stay 0.24 to 0.28). 05:30:28 (21 minutes, blazes 22 to 34): recorded stay 0.50, now go_for_food/return_for_food 5 of 5 (0.47 to 0.54). 05:34:39 (26 minutes, two blazes 9 and 11 off just drifted in): stay 5 of 5 still (0.48 to 0.56), with the leave and the food split under it (0.17 and 0.20). Added to the replay suite as pocket-nether-no-food-blazes-drifting-25592 (the 05:30:28 asking; go_for_food 5 of 5). Tests (test/pocket-wait.test.js): the trial's pocket with nothing to eat and the trip held says the trip on the leave and offers no second trip. The same pocket after a restart, its record begun thirteen minutes after the seal with no pocketOutAt and a pre-584 stance, says 13 minutes and the blaze it was sealed against. Two blazes drifting 22 to 4 to 22 unseen are said with their range and never in sight, priced as should they all come. The stay says health does not come back and that neither daylight nor health comes. With the trip's hold lapsed, the leave goes to the rung and go_for_food offers return_for_food with the portal's 280 blocks and no healing on the way. A pocket the bot was seen out of since an old seal, and one sealed again, are new waits. The 584 test of a zombie come nearer is now the mob it was sealed against (in sight when sealed), still priced among them. Each fails without the change. npm test 1881 of 1881, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 47 of 48 with the known gap. Still open: with blazes 9 and 11 blocks off, just come in, Jev still stays (a player might too, for a minute), and the trip's ten-minute hold lapses while the bot waits sealed, so the leave then goes back to the rung and leave_nether is asked again. The pocket's tunnel_out away from the blazes was not offered once in the 33 minutes (passageOut found no straight run; not looked into here). dig_in_and_fight says none of the blazes has a line in through the mouth and prices 36.8 damage in the same breath (the blaze stand's, note 577's). None of this has run live.
590. *mid-208-k-nether-4-fortress-1 (25589) and mid-242-af (25598), each held on its own pillar most of a quarter hour, the audit at 05:34 with "pillar hold" the top step (9 and 8 of 15 minutes) and encounter_stance given the same answer over and over (37 of 46, 30 of 45).* 25589: at 05:23:57, 7.9 health, hunger 17 and nothing to eat, a hoglin 8 blocks off on its warped search; pillar (0.87), up by 05:24:02 on two nether bricks at (-39, 44, 60) beside a crimson fungus's trunk, under its cap. From then to 05:53 and still going when this was written: health 7.9 throughout, no hit taken, no swing, the hoglin 4 blocks off in sight at every look, never nearer and never gone. encounter_stance every fifteen seconds (the stance's own clock: the pillar's estimate of fifteen seconds runs out, and the hoglin, facing the bot, reads as "still coming"), pillar 0.60 to 0.77 with fight 0.52 to 0.63 about one asking in four; each fight stood eight seconds on the top with nothing in reach (the charge found no route to the hoglin) and left it be as out of reach, and the next asking was pillar. turn_priority every minute, survival 0.98. 25598: at 05:14:51, 19 health, piglins without gold worn on it, pillar (0.48); up at (17, 70, -174) over a 12-block drop, hit by one crossbow bolt in its first second (to 17.8), ate, and from 05:15:17 to 05:26:58 at 20 health, nothing struck, a crossbow piglin 8.1 blocks off in sight the whole twelve minutes without a shot, three more piglins and a brute 11 to 15 blocks off out of sight, a hoglin 15 off; pillar 0.52 to 0.76 at every asking, none good 0.07 to 0.15. It ended only by the restart of 05:26:58 (the bot fell off the top reconnecting), after which it fought, went on at 05:29 and was on its fortress legs until the ghast of 05:37 (note 586's). What ends a hold: nothing up there. The stance is asked again when its estimate's seconds run out, when six health is lost, when a new mob comes within six, or when the stance fails; the pillar at its top never fails (its run faces the nearest and returns), so every fifteen seconds it is asked again and, told the same, answered the same. The ledger of note 571 records the stances and does not read them back or rest them, by design (their own options say their failures, note 521); the pillar's option said none of its own. What every asking said: "Go two blocks straight up on placed blocks and fight from there: ... the sword still reaches them", about 0 damage (25589) or 25.1 and 48.5 (25598, the crossbow piglin counted as shooting) "in the next fifteen seconds this way", and "the work waits meanwhile: it has waited 634 seconds". Nothing said the bot was already up, for how long, that nothing had been struck or lost, that the hoglin had not come nearer in ten minutes nor gone, that the sword did not reach it from the top, or what the fight chosen from up there had come to; previousStance said the hoglin was "still coming at the bot". And the way down was not among the options in either: come_down is offered from pillarDescent, which wants the feet within 0.18 of the block's middle, and mid-242-af stood 0.2 off it (x 17.2998 on block 17) for twelve minutes; and at 25589 as high as pillarHeight measures, the drop beside the top with two open cells over it, and under the fungus's cap there was no side open at head height (read from a copy of the region file: nether wart block over every side cell but the bot's own), so no height and no option; and chosen, descendPillar would have refused either for ten minutes (bot._pillarUp, the guard against undoing a pillar toward a portal). A player on a pillar fights from it or comes down: it would have struck at the hoglin at the foot if it came, or come down the two blocks it went up, and gone on. Now the hold keeps a record while the bot is on its own pillar's top (src/pillar-wait.js, survival.state.pillarWait, begun at the first look up there, a new hold after a minute off it or on another pillar): when it began, what the pillar was chosen against, the health lost up there and when, the swings (the defense's strikes) and the kills, the stances chosen up there, and each mob about with where it began, its nearest and farthest and whether it has been in sight, forgotten a minute after it was last seen. Ended, it is kept (state.pillarHolds, the last twelve) with its place, its seconds, what was struck, killed and lost, and how it ended. Up on the top the pillar's option says "Hold on the pillar's top, two up, and fight from there ... the sword reaches one that stands at the column's foot", then the hold: "Up on this pillar 10 minutes so far, chosen against the hoglin 4 blocks off. In that time: no health lost up here, nothing struck, nothing killed. The fight has been chosen up here 9 times in this hold: nothing came within the sword's reach and nothing was struck. Of the mobs about: the hoglin 4 blocks off, about for 10 minutes of the hold, 4 blocks off all that time, never nearer, in sight; it does not reach the top, and the sword does not reach it from up here. The hoglin has not come nearer, reached the bot or come within the sword's reach in 10 minutes, and it has not gone: the hold has had nothing to strike, and waiting up here has not sent it away. No daylight comes here: nothing about burns off or goes away with the hour, so a hold here ends only when the bot leaves the top. Health 7.9 does not come back at hunger 17: holding heals nothing." A shooter is said by whether it has hit the bot up there ("it shoots, and a piglin last hit the bot up here 12 minutes ago"), and with health lost earlier in the hold the nothing-comes sentence says "nothing has hurt the bot for the last 12 minutes" in place of "reached the bot"; it is said only when every mob about a minute or more has come no nearer, none reaches the top or is in the sword's reach, nothing has hurt the bot for two minutes, and no newcomer is within eight. The holds made from within sixteen blocks in the ten minutes before are said with what they came to ("Held a pillar from about here once before in the last 5 minutes, 4 minutes in all: nothing struck, nothing killed, no health lost; the last ended: off the top"), and past five minutes without a new best the rung's time (the ledger's, as note 584's stay). The fight's option says the same of the fight from the top; the state carries it (pillarSoFar); previousStance says a mob the pillar was chosen against is "still facing the bot, no nearer in the 10 minutes up on the pillar" where the hold shows that, not "still coming"; the turn's own claim says it ("Up on its pillar 11 minutes so far: no health lost up here, nothing struck; the hoglin 4 blocks off all that time, never nearer."). And the way down is offered on the bot's own pillar's top: over the block within 0.3 of its middle (the descent steps it to the middle first), as high as the pillar was raised when no side is open at head height, said as "Come down the pillar the way it went up: the block under the feet is dug out and the bot drops onto the next, about a second each, 2 blocks down to the ground it went up from; shot at meanwhile if a shooter is about. Down there the mobs that cannot reach the top can reach the bot again, and the sword reaches them", digging only as many blocks as it went up; chosen in a fight, descendPillar no longer refuses a pillar the bot raised. Nothing is decided by any of it: the stances are Jev's as before. Probed against the live model with the recorded states (the hold replayed through pillar-wait.js with the recorded mobs, health, answers and times, the mobs matched by kind and nearest distance, the new sentences put where the code puts them, come_down added as the code now offers it; the rung's line left out, its clock not recoverable from the records), 5 runs each: 25589 at 05:34:31, pillar 5 of 5 as recorded (0.65 to 0.70, fight 0.29 to 0.34) and 5 of 5 now (0.57 to 0.66, fight 0.32 to 0.39, come_down 0.01 to 0.02); 25598 at 05:26:55, pillar 5 of 5 as recorded (0.60 to 0.71, none good 0.14 to 0.19) and 5 of 5 now (0.78 to 0.83, come_down 0.05 to 0.07, none good 0.05 to 0.07). turn_priority at 25589's 05:34:59 with the claim's new sentence: survival 5 of 5 as recorded (0.98) and now (0.95 to 0.96). So the facts are said and the way down is there, and Jev still holds: told the hold has cost nothing, it keeps the option that costs nothing, and the ways off are each priced as every mob about coming at once (25598: the fight 59 damage, the work 50 from the crossbow piglin "the first hit ends it", the retreat "no way out") against twelve minutes in which none came and nothing was hurt. Tried and not kept, as sentences Jev did not move on (5 of 5 pillar each): the hold's mobs said in the work's option; each mob's line in the header ("it has come no nearer"); the work's shots said as "should it shoot"; come_down priced "should they all come at once". None of them is in the change. Not added to the replay suite: there is no answer here yet to hold it to. Tests (test/pillar-wait.test.js): 25589's hold replayed through the stance step, eleven minutes at a quarter minute an asking with the fight chosen one in four: the pillar says the hold as above, the fight says its nine choosings came to nothing, the state and the claim say it; a zombie come from 8 to 4 while the bot swung twice and lost 3 is said so and the nothing-comes sentence is not; off the top the hold is kept and the next pillar two blocks off says it, and at the foot the pillar is said as before; on its own pillar 0.2 off the middle under a cap closed at head height, come_down is offered, two blocks. test/pillar-recovery.test.js: in a fight on a pillar the bot raised, 0.2 off the middle, the descent steps to the middle and digs; out of a fight it still refuses. Each fails without the change. npm test 1884 of 1884, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 46 of 47 with the known gap. Still open: why Jev holds a safe pillar with nothing coming is the ways off priced as the whole crowd arriving, the same shape as note 584's leave, not changed here; why the crossbow piglin in sight at 8 blocks did not shoot for twelve minutes, and why the fight's charge found no route to a hoglin 4 blocks off that could not reach the bot either, are not found; the stance is still asked every fifteen seconds while the hold stands; the rung's budget (note 571) is watched from the work's step, which does not run while survival has the turn, so nothing above the stance asks after a hold's minutes; none of this has run live.
593. *mid-242-ag (25594), "shot by Skeleton" at 05:50:46 on a fresh world, standing at (29.3, 70, 61.3) for thirty-three seconds while a skeleton 4.5 to 7 blocks off shot it every three or four seconds, 20 to 17 to 14.9 to 11.1 to 8.6 to 6.5 to 4.3 to 5.3 to 2.3 to none; turn_priority chose survival six times (0.84 to 0.98), and no stance was asked, nothing moved, swung or raised the shield.* From 05:50:13 the survival layer had the turn and its step returned at once, twenty-one passes a second ("[loop] spinning"), the last action reported still the leave_shelter of 05:49:52 until the two meals its vitals ate. What the step did: the skeleton was a threat at hand (immediateThreat), so stepOnce took its emergency branch, and a saved shelter lay within three blocks: a pocket the bot had sealed on 2026-09-26 at (31, 70, 61), in this state's shelter list. By that branch's rule a prepared site so near is sealed rather than the mob answered, with refugeStep given the method (saved_shelter). refugeStep dropped the site (a pocket not inside it with water or lava under it, shelter.wetBelow, is not gone back to: the pool the bot had been filling buckets at), and with a method given and no site, its failure (`failed`, "the saved shelter is out of reach") rested the method and returned true, which the emergency read as its answer: 508 refusals in the saved survival state, the last at 05:50:45.9, 0.1 seconds before the death. Nothing reported, so the step had "acted", the arbiter held survival, and the stance question was never reached. The candidates looked at: no stance was held (the seal stance of 05:49:37 had run until 05:49:52 and its fifteen seconds were out, stanceHeld null); the ledger's holds do not touch the stance (note 571: stances are recorded, not read back) and shelter_method is not in the ledger; the claim's own words ("the stance is asked next") were true of the claim, not of the step. The leave at 05:49:52 and 05:49:54 was a second fault: Jev chose seal at 05:49:37 against the skeleton, the pocket closed at 05:49:52, and pocket_next's leave, chosen at 05:48:59 in the shaft pocket three blocks off, was held ninety seconds by a key that did not name the pocket (the watcher, night, a bed near, the options), so the new pocket was opened the moment it closed, twice, with no question. The same signature elsewhere: saved-shelter refusals in the hundreds or thousands are the emergency's (the night question rests the option three minutes after one), and 17 archived states hold five or more; mid-235-p-nether-4-fortress-1 (25585) was "fireballed by Ghast" at 01:27:49 after twenty-two seconds of the same spin, its last report an out_of_sight stance at 01:27:27, 897 refusals, the last 0.25 seconds before the death, over the lava sea (wetBelow counts lava); 25582 (3,214, around mid-202-k's zombie death at 11:30:41, not confirmed as this), 25594 at 11:31 on 2026-09-27 (2,724), 25600 at 20:05 (2,209), 25581, 25587, 25599 and 25590 (125 to 303) with no death within two minutes; logs with runs of "[hurt] hit with no survival response" of three to seven (mid-235-q-nether-1-fortress-3, mid-235-p-nether-4-fortress-3, mid-242-aa-nether-3, mid-244-ae and others) are not all this cause and are not looked at here. Now (survival.js stepOnce): with Jev reachable, a threat at hand is the encounter's question (flee, the stance asked unless one holds), whatever shelter is near; the saved site's seal is the rule's answer only without Jev (note 549), and there, refugeStep's failure of a method its caller gave returns false, so the rules answer the mob (flee) instead of the refusal standing as the answer. pocket_next's held answer is kept with the pocket it was given in (its origin in the key): another pocket is asked. Tests (test/emergency-stance.test.js, the recorded ground: the bot at (29.7, 70, 61.7), the 2026-09-26 pocket at (31, 70, 61) over a pool, the skeleton 4.5 blocks off, 14.9 health): three survival passes go to the stance each time and never to the site's seal; the stance question is asked with its options and saved_shelter is not tried and refused; without Jev the seal is tried, refused and the mob answered, and refugeStep with the method given says false; a stay held in one pocket is held there and asked again in a pocket three blocks off. Each fails without the change. npm test 1887 of 1887, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 47 of 48 with the known gap. Still open: nothing checks in general that a survival turn that says it acted did anything (a step that reports nothing, moves nothing and asks nothing while a threat hits the bot); the stance question does not name the prepared site near as a place to seal, so a half-standing old pocket is used only when the bot stands in its cell (sealHere finds it by origin); the stale leave_shelter label kept on the status for thirty seconds by a step that reported nothing; none of this has run live.
594. *mid-243-ae (25590, 74 minutes, no milestone, one death) and mid-244-ah (25581, 54 minutes, no milestone): fresh worlds past their first three days, where a player is through a portal within the hour. Each spent more than half its time on one thing the Nether needed, gotten the slow way: a water bucket, then food.* mid-243-ae, minute by minute from its status lines and flight records, began at 04:53 with iron armor, an iron pickaxe, 139 coal and a water bucket. 04:53 to 05:08: upkeep wood_reserve at y 19 (0.45), then the stone pickaxe rung's log, climbed straight up from y 25 to 92 (10 minutes, two restarts in it); the iron pickaxe was made at 05:08:46. 05:09 to 05:17: reach nether. It made buckets and chose cast_at_lava (0.24): stairs down to lava, five lava buckets, and a frame cast on the mountain at (51, 109, -33), 7 of 10 in by 05:17. At 05:17:56 it stood on the mold's top at (51, 113, -34), walking four blocks over to (55, 112, -33), and way_down offered dig_down ("1 block to dig (1 cobblestone), about 1 seconds ... where a walk goes on", 0.40) and ride_water ("the bucket comes back empty and the water stays up here (any water refills it)", 0.54). Its only water bucket went into the ride. No other water was in reach: a copy of the region file holds one water source between x 30 and 110, z -90 and -20, y 95 and 118, the one poured, on the pillar's top, with 170 cells of its runoff below. 05:18 to 05:54 (36 minutes): the cast asked for a water bucket. collectWater found no source with a dry cell to stand on that a quarter-second survey could reach, and explore('water') took over. It asked search_heading about 30 times and swam its own runoff 181 status lines ("water from 18 to 18"). A skeleton (05:27, a pocket at 13.7), two food hunts (05:29, and 05:42 to 05:45 up to 200 blocks east) and a creeper (05:41) came between. portal_method was asked again at 05:51 ("Needs: a water bucket (none carried)", nothing of where water is or how long it had been looked for), cast_frame 0.52. At 05:54:18 a skeleton killed it, and the 3 lava buckets, 28 iron ingots and the kit dropped. 05:54 to 06:01: a shaft pocket at the respawn at night, stay five times: the audit's "wait in shelter". 06:01 to 06:12: from nothing, wood, a stone pickaxe, a stone sword, wool for a bed, the iron pickaxe rung. The clock: 36 of 74 minutes on the water, 15 on a climb for a log, 9 on the lava and the cast, 7 in the pocket after the death. A player does not pour away the water a cast needs to get down four blocks; it digs the one block. mid-244-ah: 04:46 to 04:54, the bed's wool underground (a fight to 3.9 health, a pocket, food), the bed made at 04:54. 04:55 to 05:35 (40 minutes): reach nether, the crossing kit short 40 food points of 40. top_up_food was chosen at 05:01:40 (0.65; cows 114 east, sheep 116 south), at 05:08:49 (0.53; "a cow in view, 10 blocks off", 1 cow 35 southeast, 4 sheep 77 south) and at 05:21:06 (0.58; "22 working minutes have gone to it at this crossing, from 2 to 0"). Each time the option said "The food known (the search does not go to it first)"; the known-food way (top_up_food_near) was offered only with a frame begun. The step, hunt_food_for_nether, was explore('animals'), and no block is named animals: the search had nothing to walk to and walked its legs by heading, x 70 to 618, z -493 to 95, and twice "Could not find reachable animals after 128 exploration steps". The kills were the survival layer's stock hunt (11, in four bursts). It ate each raw, at hunger 15 to 19 from the walking and swimming, while turn_priority gave the turn to the work, "hunt food for nether", 30-odd times (0.89 at 05:13). Food carried stayed 0 to 3 for 35 minutes and was 27 (9 raw beef) at 05:33, with 126 coal and two furnaces carried all along. A player kills the cow ten blocks off and the sheep, and cooks them: that is over forty. 05:35 to 05:39: buckets, a frame at (332, 67, -271), cross_now (0.59), the lava; then skeletons, water, and the drowned at 3.1 health (not looked at here). The rule behind each: the option that spent the time said something untrue or left the route out. The ride said any water refills the bucket where none was in reach, and said nothing of the cast. The food top-up promised a hunt that only walked, counted a cow as 16 points where its kill counts 6, and offered the food in view only once a frame was begun. Now: way_down's ride_water says the water stays on the top, so many blocks over where the ride lands, and only a climb back takes it again. It says the nearest other water source within 48 blocks and where (or that none is in view there, so water must be found first), and what the work in hand needs the bucket for: a portal being cast from lava and water, its frame and how many of 10 are in (waterBucketUse), or a planned step that uses one. The crossing kit offers the known food as its own way (top_up_food_near) with no frame begun too: the nearest by the trip there and back here. Chosen, it walks to a herd, a village, the plot or the chest as before, and hunts an animal in view itself (foraging.hunt; it used to leave that to the survival layer). The open search hunts a grown cow, sheep, rabbit or mooshroom in view within 32 blocks before it walks on, and says so. A kill that fails (behind cover, no way to it) is kept two minutes as the survival layer keeps it (failedPrey) and is not said as food in view meanwhile. Meat on a trip is said as the kill leaves it, raw (healing RAW_MEAT_POINTS: a cow about 6, a sheep 3, a hoglin 9), with what cooking makes of it beside. top_up_cook is offered where raw food is carried with fuel and a furnace (or 8 cobblestone for one): the points as carried and once cooked, and the seconds at a furnace put down here (the code's fallback takes it before the other food ways). Each way at food says its own minutes, not the others'. Probed against the live model with the recorded askings, the new texts put where the code puts them (the kit's from crossingKitReady on a bot where the recorded one stood), 5 runs each. mid-243-ae's way_down at 05:17:56: ride_water 5 of 5 as recorded (0.54 to 0.61), dig_down 5 of 5 now (0.72 to 0.75). mid-244-ah's crossing_kit at 05:08:49: top_up_food 5 of 5 as recorded (0.51 to 0.54), top_up_food_near 5 of 5 now (0.34 to 0.40; the open search 0.21 to 0.23). At 05:21:06, the cows 184 blocks off for about 12 raw (32 cooked): top_up_food 5 of 5 as recorded (0.56 to 0.60) and still 5 of 5 now (0.31 to 0.37, near 0.18 to 0.21). The open search it keeps now hunts what it meets. Added to the replay suite: way-down-ride-spends-cast-water-243ae (dig_down 5 of 5), crossing-kit-cow-in-view-no-frame-244ah (top_up_food_near 5 of 5). Tests: way-down.test.js (the ride says the water left up top, no other water in 48 or the nearest, and the cast's need; waterBucketUse for a cast with blocks left, none once cast, none for a mined frame, a planned step's; live, the walk off a top asks with those words and looks 48 blocks for water); crossing-food.test.js (mid-244-ah's 05:08 state: the known food offered with no frame, back here, "about 6 of the 40 points short as raw meat, about 16 once cooked", and taken it hunts the cow; the open search hunts the cow in view before it walks; a cow it could not get to is kept and the sheep become the known food; 9 beef with coal and a furnace: top_up_cook with 27 as carried and 72 once cooked, none without fuel; the open search's 22 minutes are not the known food's). Each fails without the change. The note 527 test now expects the known food with no frame begun, and its trip counted raw. npm test 1922 of 1922, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 53 of 54 with the known gap. Still open: the water search itself. A source on a pillar's top with only its own runoff around it, no dry cell to stand on that the survey reaches, is never scooped, and the search swims the runoff with no bound. portal_method says "a water bucket (none carried)" with nothing of where water is or how long it has been searched for. By 05:51 the cast was asked about a new frame at (76, 109, -55), 0 of 10, the frame with 7 in left behind (not looked into). The survival layer's stock hunt eats its raw kills to hold hunger at eighteen while the search walks. mid-242-ah (25594) had "reach nether: food near frame" 4 of 30 minutes at 06:36, 2,797 blocks walked for 87 net (not looked into). The drowned death is not looked into. None of this has run live.

577. *No blaze rod taken in any trial: the fortress stage's blaze deaths (mid-208-k-fortress-4 on 25584, 04:08:29, take_cover twice and burned from 10.6 by blazes twenty to twenty-five off; mid-235-p-nether-4-fortress-4, a wither skeleton at 1.8 and blazes eight to twenty-five off; mid-242-ac-nether-3-fortress-1, a blaze 3.6 off never struck from a bunker; mid-242-ab-nether-3-fortress-2; notes 548, 557), taken to the arena.* The arena's server folder was gone: `scripts/trials/arena-start.sh` makes `.test-combat` in the main checkout (the trials' 26.1.2 jar and Java 25, a flat world, port 25574, the console pipe and `logs/arena-console.log`; `ARENA_DIR`/`ARENA_PORT` for a second one), and `arena.js` finds it from any worktree. The bot is no longer opped (only a watcher is). The arena's gamerules had been refused since 26.1 renamed them ("Incorrect argument", `doMobSpawning` and the rest), so drills ran with natural spawns; they are `minecraft:spawn_mobs` and so on now, fire keeps its own ticking (at `fire_spread_radius_around_player 0` no fire ever burns out and the floor stays walled with it), and the new drills have `mob_griefing` on, so a fireball that misses lights the ground as in every trial. New drills, with the trials' kit (iron sword, shield, iron armour, iron pickaxe, cobblestone and netherrack; `FORTRESS_KIT`), no nudge (a blaze that sees the bot keeps off and shoots): `blaze_fortress_spawner` (a seven-wide brick floor along a netherrack cliff over a lava sea, a live blaze spawner at its end, three blazes by it twenty-one to twenty-five off), `blaze_wither_fortress` (the same, a wither skeleton at two blocks, blazes ten and twenty-one off), `blaze_pit_above` (the bot two deep in a pit, three blazes five to eight over it), `blaze_open_eight` (one blaze eight off on open ground). `ARENA_PREFER` answers named options wherever offered (one stand measured at a time), every answer is logged with what was offered, and beats say each mob's height. Measured first (`scripts/blaze-probe.js`, a probe player in iron on the arena server): a blaze glows (metadata 16, bit 0) three seconds, throws three fireballs at about 2.9, 3.2 and 3.5 seconds, stops, and rests five; at five blocks with the shield down, 15.5 damage in 31 seconds, 8 of it the fire (a fireball is 2.5 through iron and five seconds alight at one a second); with the shield timed to the glow, 1 in 36; held up, 0 in 36 facing it, and from three or five blazes spread thirty degrees apart about one fireball in thirty still lands. In the drills the hurts said why the bot died where it stood behind a raised shield: the fire at its feet, lit by its own blocked and missed volleys (in-fire 0.4 a half second and relit every moment), and walks to the blazes and the cage refused outright: `safeFromHostiles` refuses every cell nearer a shooter in sight, so with blazes about every route toward them was "no path" (a scratch run: the same floor walked in 3.7 seconds with no blaze about, no path with one). Now (blaze-stand.js): `shieldVolley`, a reflex, stops and holds the shield facing the middle of the blazes whose volley is due (2.4 seconds into a glow to a second after it ends), held through the whole of it (lowered and raised again it takes a quarter second to block), walking on crouched behind it when the way on is where it faces; `putOutFlames` punches out the fire the body stands in; `closeIn` walks in (onFoot, sprinting, the blazes claimed as the fight's so the walk may near them) to ground within a sword's reach of the nearest blaze over ground with a block to spare from any drop or lava, strikes a biter at arm's length first, then the blaze struck last while it is in reach, picks up rods near between volleys, and is asked again after 45 seconds, a rod, or six health gone; `sortie` steps two blocks out of a hole or off a wall to a blaze just past the sword and back (the bunker's hold, holdBeat); `breakSpawner` walks to the cage behind the shield, breaks it with the pickaxe and then closes in; a hole beside the cage with its mouth toward it (`spawnerHoleSite`); the spawner's cell takes rock at its back where there is no ceiling; the bunker's hold and its rod pickup go between volleys, not waiting for no blaze in sight (at a spawner there always is one). Offered to Jev as `close_in`, `break_spawner` and `dig_in_at_spawner` beside the old stands, in the hunt (hunt_target) and the stance (encounter_stance), each with what the arena measured of it, every drill it was measured in, the one like the fight at hand first (`MEASURED`, by scene): at the live spawner, three runs each, close_in 6 killed, 2 rods, 21 damage a run on the median, 1 death; break_spawner 2, 1 rod, 34.1, 2 deaths; dig_in_at_spawner 1, 1 rod, 29, 1 death; dig_in_and_fight 1, no rod, 3.1, none; back_to_wall none, 34.7, 2 deaths; the fight in the open (hunt_N) 1, no rod, 39.9, 2 deaths; leaving them (Jev's own stances after, five runs of the old code) 1, no rod, 21.1, none; with the wither skeleton, close_in and break_spawner 3 deaths in 3 each (before the biter came first), back_to_wall none in 3, 13.1, no rod. Against one blaze in the open close_in killed it both times for 2.1 damage. Jev's free choice, five runs a drill (before: main at 519b4d7 with the new drills and rules; blaze_swarm_wall before from cd652d9): fortress spawner 0 deaths, 0 rods, 21.1 damage, 1 killed, every hunt deferred, to 1 death, 2 rods, 6.5, 4 killed (close_in, dig_in_and_fight); wither 1 death, 0 rods, 36.7, 6 killed, to 2 deaths, 2 rods, 33.9, 8 killed (close_in and back_to_wall); pit 0 rods, 6.5 both (close_in is not offered: no ground reaches them from the pit, and every hunt is deferred); open eight 2 rods, 12.4, 4 killed, to 2 rods, 0 damage, 5 killed, close_in 5 of 5; swarm wall (diamond kit) 1 rod, 3.4, to 2 rods, 3.4. Eight rods in twenty-five runs where there were three, and three deaths where there was one: a live spawner with three or more blazes is still lost more often than a rod is taken with this kit, and Jev is told so in the figures. Tests (test/blaze-stand.test.js): the volley read from the glow, the flames in every column the box touches and punched out, the cage offered with rock at its back and a hole beside it mouth toward it, close_in offered only with a shield and a blaze over ground, the measured rows said with the scene's first; the arena's rules and fills. npm test 1884 of 1884, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow. Still open: why a shield held up and facing, the server saying it is in use in the off hand, still took fireballs in the drills that the probe blocked (up 27 seconds, blazes within 31 degrees of the facing); the claim that lets a walk near the blazes lapses under fourteen health (mob-policy fitToFight), so a stand chosen hurt cannot walk to them; burning with nothing to put it out, and the retreat not out of sight (the coordinator's mid-208-k-nether-4-fortress-2), are the fire and stance layers'; the stage save .trial-checkpoints/stages/fortress/mid-208-k-nether-4-050657 is a drill not yet built; nothing here has run in a trial.

591. *mid-242-ab-nether-4 (25587), a fortress 102 blocks off in its last minutes and never reached: fortress_approach offered walk_route ("the pathfinder found no route toward it from here", chosen 0.41 at 05:42:04), tunnel and keep_searching; fortress_leg offered restock_blocks and return_for_blocks, none good at 0.61 to 0.71 six times in a row, then back_to_fortress and cross_level, round and round ("flipping find_fortress <-> cross_toward"); and mid-242-aa-nether-4 (25584, 04:59 to 05:08), the two questions churning every few seconds with a fortress in view.* What lay between, read from 25587's region files: the bot at (203.5, 47, -486.3) stood in a pocket at the lip of a pit nine blocks across down to lava at y 21 to 31; west and north of it, a wall of netherrack some ninety blocks thick at y 47 with gold and quartz ore through it; past that, a crimson forest's open air with floors at y 37 to 48, where the bot had stood at 05:34 before a leg took it east; then the fortress's near end at x 128 to 150, z -572 to -554, its pillars rising from the lava and its floors at y 57. No lava sea lay between at the bot's height, only rock and the pit. The straight crossing at y 47 (bridging.js surveyCrossing, the whole line) to the nearest floor is 135 cells: 158 blocks of rock dug and 54 laid over open air, 12 of them over lava, ending 11 under the floor. To the nearest brick, a pillar in the forest at y 47, it is 142 cells: 205 dug and 36 laid, 13 over lava. At the floor's own height, y 58, the line meets lava 42 cells along. The bot carried no pickaxe (its iron one had worn out) and no block a span is laid with (its 16 gravel and 5 wart blocks are neither), but it had four oak logs and a crafting table. 225 netherrack could be dug from ground walked to within sixteen blocks, and 3,871 more lay within sixteen out of reach. Why none good: restock_blocks was "Dig 29 blocks to lay spans with here ... about 65 seconds ... The longest leg short of blocks needs 29 ... The leg is chosen again after": blocks for a leg, priced as dug by hand, when netherrack dug by hand drops nothing (the last restock "gained 0"). Nothing said the blocks could take it to the fortress, or how far. return_for_blocks was 528 blocks back to the portal. The fortress was not among the leg's ways: Jev had left it from that spot over walk route and tunnel, and going back from there is not offered (note 541). A player makes a pickaxe from the logs, mines a stack of netherrack, and tunnels and bridges straight at the fortress. Two of the four legs had also rested on "nether gold ore in the way": the crossing's rock (bridging.js NATURAL) left out the Nether's ores, so a crossing, a leg's span and the fortress map's digs stopped at ore as at a wall (1,531 such lines in the midgame logs). Now the ores are rock to the crossing and the map. fortress_approach offers blocks_then_cross in the Nether when four things hold: the crossing straight at the fortress at this height, surveyed to its end (up to 192 cells), comes nearer; it lays more blocks than are carried; a pickaxe is carried or can be made from the wood carried; and blocks can be dug here. It is said with the pickaxe (made first where none is carried), the crossing's blocks laid (how many over lava), the rock dug and the cells, the blocks carried, how many are mined (up to a stack) out of how many to be had here, the seconds with the pickaxe it will have, the crossing with them (where it ends, what stops it, about how long), the fortress's floors from where it ends, the ghasts and blazes in sight, and the minutes in all. Chosen, the blocks are gathered as the restock gathers them and the crossing is laid to its end. fortress_leg offers the same when the fortress in view is off its floors, set aside or left. Taken there, it is carried out rather than the ways in being asked again, so it is offered from where Jev left the fortress too, and that is said. walk_route is not offered where its survey found no route; that is said in the state (walkRoute). The pickaxe is now a fact of fortress_approach. keep_searching says the minutes searching and which legs from here rest, and why. restock_blocks says the pickaxe (made first from the wood carried, or that none can be) and is priced with the pickaxe it will have. Measured on the saved world, with a bot over 25587's region files running findFortressStep from the recorded spot with the recorded inventory and the pathfinder answering no path as it did: fortress_approach offers blocks_then_cross ("Mine netherrack for blocks here first, then go straight at the fortress with them, 98 blocks off and 11 blocks up. No pickaxe is carried ... a wooden pickaxe is made first from what is carried (4 logs, a crafting table) ... The crossing to its end lays 54 blocks (12 over lava) and digs 158 of rock in 135 cells; 0 carried. Mined first: 54 from the 225 ... about 29 seconds. Then, with them: ... about 184 seconds ... It ends 96 blocks nearer ... About 4 minutes in all"), tunnel and keep_searching, with walk_route said in the state. fortress_leg, with the fortress left from there and the legs resting, offers it beside restock_blocks and return_for_blocks. Probed against the live model, 5 asks each, the recorded states with the options as now asked: fortress_leg at 05:42:05, blocks_then_cross 5 of 5 (0.44 to 0.49, none good 0.34 to 0.41; as recorded, restock_blocks 4 and none good 1); fortress_approach at 05:42:04, keep_searching 5 of 5 (0.47 to 0.52, blocks_then_cross 0.31 to 0.35; as recorded, walk_route 5 of 5). Variants of the approach's text all stayed keep_searching: without the fireball's push, with the cells in rock said, shorter, with the pickaxe fact out, with the search's minutes in the state, and with keep_searching's legs said or not. With the unmeasured tunnel left out, it was blocks_then_cross 4 of 5. So from that spot the bot now leaves the fortress at the approach and takes the crossing at the legs question that follows, which runs it. Both states are added to the replay suite, the approach as a known gap. mid-242-aa-nether-4 was another shape: 114 blocks carried, the bot in a tunnel at y 39 on a shelf over the lava sea, the fortress's bridge at y 73 standing in open air 34 blocks above; walk_route was offered there too as "found no route", and that is the only part this change touches there. Tests (test/fortress-wall.test.js, on the ground as the region files had it: test/fixtures/fortress-wall-mid-242-ab.json, 2,755 columns from y 20 to 70; each fails without its change): the crossing to the pillar digs the gold ore and reaches it (it had stopped 93 cells along), and the crossing to the floor is 135 cells, 158 dug, 54 laid. fortress_approach from the recorded spot offers blocks_then_cross with those figures and not walk_route, with walkRoute and the pickaxe in the state and keep_searching saying the legs resting. fortress_leg, with the fortress left there, offers it with the set-aside said, and says so where Jev left over it; restock_blocks says the pickaxe. Chosen with a pickaxe, 54 blocks are mined and 54 laid, and the bot stands under the fortress's floor. npm test 1883 of 1883, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 47 of 49, the known gap and the approach here as a new one. Still open: Jev leaves the fortress at the approach from there; the tunnel is offered unmeasured, dug by hand where no pickaxe is carried; the crossing is at the bot's height (here the floor's height met lava), and one that ends under a floor leaves the way up to the next approach; the netherrack the crossing digs and picks up is not counted toward its blocks; the long crossing has run only in the test's world; none of this has run live.

592. *mid-243-af-nether-1 (25587), "tried to swim in lava" at 05:45:38 from 20 health, main at 9464c93 (note 580 live): into the lava sea off the gravel beach at (-232, 33, 56).* At 05:45:09 Jev chose fortress_leg leg_south (0.30); the pathfinder's partial route walked the leg down to the sea's edge and stood the bot at (-231.5, 33, 56.5) on gravel at (-232, 32, 56) (the flight frame of 05:45:33.78 has it underfoot), and the stall at (-216.5, 36, 26.5) twelve seconds before was thirty blocks off and is not in it. At 05:45:33.78 the leg's walk found no route and the crossing began unasked (nether-travel.js crossToward, surveyCrossing 32 cells, 32 to lay, 82 blocks carried); bridge_step held forward crouched from 05:45:33.83, and its first block, the bot's last netherrack, went in at (-232, 32, 57) against the gravel's south face (the saved world has it there and the gravel gone). That gravel rested on the lava sea (lava at (-232, 31, 56)): gravel, sand and the other blocks that fall stay where they are generated until a block beside them changes, and then fall if nothing holds them (FallingBlock, isFree: air, fire, a liquid). The gravel went into the lava and the body with it: y 32.5 at 05:45:34.01, falling, the move's lava check refusing ("lava at (-232, 31, 57) ahead") with the body already going down, 29.7 at the lowest. Not the pathfinder's move, not a recovery, not a step onto magma or a flowing cell or the lava's edge: the crossing's own block, beside the floor. Note 580's rule did not see it because nothing was wrong with where the feet stood: a whole block of gravel holds the feet at 33, over the lava's 31.9, and standsInLava counted it dry, as it was; no rule anywhere knew a floor could fall. The way out: body_way was asked at 17.9 (05:45:34.4) and 15.8 (05:45:34.9); pillar_out was taken twice (0.71, then 0.49 with none good 0.34), "rise 2 to the way out 3 blocks off ... at (-233, 33, 54) ... about 1.5 seconds of blocks", beside to_dry_ground (the same cell, 7.5 seconds) and back_the_way_came ((-232, 33, 56), 5 seconds). None of the three was real. pillarUp stops at once in lava (lava round the cells the body rises through, nothing solid under it), so pillar_out was the walk at (-233, 33, 54), and every dry cell near stood on the beach's gravel with its top at 33, a block over the lava's top (the sea's top cell at 31, its surface at 31.9). In prismarine-physics on the saved beach (the physics the bot's own client moves by, and the server takes its positions from): holding jump alone the body bobs with its feet between 31.4 and 31.6; pressed against a wall, the push out of a liquid (0.3 up a tick while it presses a wall with room over the body) lifts it from 30.1 to 32.4 in about a second, and no higher than 32.6; onto a floor level with the lava's top it comes out in 1.1 seconds, onto the gravel a block higher never, bouncing between 31.2 and 32.6 for ten seconds. The body went 2 blocks west in 4 seconds and burned from 17.9 to 3.4 in the lava, then the fire lava sets took the rest (the flight: 32.3 at the end, "on fire"). back_the_way_came's "last dry footing" was the open cell over where the gravel had been. And no way said what its seconds cost: the state said 4.2 a second and 4.3 seconds to death, the ways said seconds only. Now: terrain.js hangingFloor reads the column of falling blocks a floor is the top of and where it goes when it falls (into lava, water, or a fall onto what holds with the column under the body), and floorDrops says whether a block laid or dug at a cell drops it (the six faces of the column's lowest block, the one under it excepted). The crossing (bridging.js span and crossAlong) lays and digs nothing that drops the floor stood on into lava or a fall of half the health ("Not laid at (-232, 32, 57): the gravel underfoot at (-232, 32, 56) rests on lava: a block laid or dug beside it drops it, and the body with it, into the lava"), and surveyCrossing stops there and says so, so cross_toward's stretch is zero there and is not walked; the pathfinder (movement.js dropsFloor) refuses a move that lays or digs such a block while the body stands on the floor or before it steps onto it, a block laid under the body on a jump excepted. Walking on such a floor is unchanged: it drops nothing. The ways out of lava (survival.js lavaWays): what a swim reaches is ground whose floor is no higher than the lava's top (lavaTop, swimReach: the top lava cell of the run up from the feet; with lava one deep it is the old "a jump rises one"), and lavaExit ranks by that; to_dry_ground and to_water are offered only for such a cell; the seconds are the longer of the way across at 0.4 blocks a second and the rise at 0.8 (jump in lava, 0.04 a tick with the motion halved); pillar_out in lava is now what gets a body out of a sea like this: pressed against a block beside the top lava cell of its column or one beside it, jump held, the push lifts it, and as the feet clear the cell a block goes into the lava under them (lavaFill, blockIntoLava), said with the block pressed against, the cell, the seconds in the lava and the dry ground a step from the block, if any; every way says the health its seconds in the lava cost at the rate through the armor worn and the fifteen seconds of burning after with no water in the Nether ("about 7.2 health in the lava at 4.5 a second, then up to 15 burning after it (no water to put it out in the Nether): about 22.2 in all, more than the 17.9 health the bot has unless it heals on the way"), or "death before it is out"; back_the_way_came says when nothing is under the footing now, and whether a swim reaches it. The code's fallback order is to_water, to_dry_ground, pillar_out, back_the_way_came, swim_up. body_way's option definitions and guidance say what a swim reaches (docs/decisions.md regenerated). Tests (test/lava-gravel.test.js, on the beach as the region file saved at 05:46 has it, x -238 to -226, y 28 to 34, z 48 to 62, the gravel put back and the span's block taken out for the world before the crossing: test/fixtures/lava-gravel-mid-243-af.json): the gravel underfoot rests on the lava and the gravel a block north on netherrack; from where the bot stood the survey toward the leg's target stops at its first cell and says the gravel, and the span lays nothing and throws the same (from the held gravel north the span is surveyed seven cells as before); the pathfinder offers no block laid against gravel resting on a lava pool under the body and does with stone under it; in the sea as the save has it (body at y 30.1, 17.9 health) no swim onto the gravel is offered, pillar_out is offered against the netherrack at (-232, 31, 55) into (-232, 31, 56), priced, with the gravel at (-232, 33, 57) a step up, and back_the_way_came says its footing is gone; and, the body moved by prismarine-physics at twenty ticks a second, pillar_out puts the block in and the body stands on it at y 32 after 0.8 seconds in the lava (priced at 1.6), where the swim at (-233, 33, 54) as the old ways walked it spends 2.5 of four seconds in the lava and never gets onto the gravel. Each of 2 to 5 fails without the change (1 without terrain.js). A replay case (body-lava-sea-under-gravel-beach-243af) asks the new ways: pillar_out 5 of 5. npm test 1884 of 1884, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 46 of 47 with the known gap, and the new case. Still open: the rest of the digs (tunneling.js, bunker, mining, the way down's dig_down) do not look for a floor that falls beside what they dig; hangingFloor reads the lava under the column as where the body goes and not how far the lava is under an air gap; the pillar out of a pit on from the block is not done (the next question asks from the block); whether 26.1's own server-side fluid height lets a body a little higher than prismarine-physics does is not measured; the crossing after a failed leg walk is still taken unasked (note 568); none of this has run live.
595. *mid-244-bb (25581), burned to death at 06:09:22 in a cave at y 12 to 20 on a fresh world, a water bucket carried.* The fire was lava's, not a flame or a skeleton's arrow (the five skeletons were 26 to 63 blocks off, none in sight). Scooping lava buckets (fill_bucket's scoop), the bot went into the pool at (160, 13, 95) at 06:09:07, took four hurts of lava through full iron (20 to 12.3), and body_way's to_dry_ground (0.80, then 0.66) had it out onto the dry cell at (159, 12, 95) by 06:09:09.5; lava sets fifteen seconds of fire, and at a health a second 12.3 was death at about 06:09:22 unless water put it out. Why the body question came late: at 06:09:09.5 the body was dropping into the dry cell, its box over the open cell (the cell under its feet was air, the floor a block lower), and fireWays offered the bucket only where the cell under the body's middle was a block and the feet's cell air, so burning out was the one way. Note 560 holds the one way as a choice (fifteen seconds or four health): "[one way] body_way: burn out" in the log, not asked or recorded. Held, the arbiter's fire reflex stood down (observeReflexes, burnLeft) and the survival step skipped the fire, and survival_priority was asked and chose obtain_food toward a sheep 54 blocks off (06:09:10, 0.82), the fire in none of its facts or of turn_priority's. At 8.3 the hold ended (four down) and body_way was asked on the cave floor with both ways: burn_out 0.43 against douse_bucket 0.33, told the bucket "puts the fire out at once ... about a second" and burning out "about 11 seconds of fire left, a health a second ..., about 8.3 health, all the health the bot has. Asked again if health falls 4 more". That was held to 4.3 (06:09:17.8), when the bot was in the air walking up a step at (136.05, 15.59, 100.44), its box's edge on the stone of (135, 14, 100) and the cell under its middle open: the bucket was not offered again, burning out was the one way again, taken unasked (the log's "[one way]" said once a minute) and held; obtain_food was chosen at 4.3 and 3.3. The next question was at 0.3 (06:09:21.7, burn_out 0.45 against 0.34), four down from 4.3. The prices: the fire left was right (10.6 seconds at 8.3 from the last lava hurt, 2.4 at 0.3; it burned out at about 06:09:24.5), and 1 health a second through armour is the game's; what was missing was the sum. Neither option said the bot dies of the burning, or when (8.3 seconds, before the fire ends), nor what the bucket spares; "about a second" was the pour and taking the water back together. A burning body did not always get the body question first: while a burn-out was held, and it was held even as the one way, every other survival question went ahead without the fire. Now: the pour goes onto the block under the body's box, the middle first, then the box's edge, and from the air down to two below the feet (vitals.js pourFloor, used by fireWays and douse; the look at the top of that block), so both recorded spots offer the bucket. A held burn-out keeps the ways it was chosen among, and a way not among them on offer now ends the hold (body.js held, looked at every quarter second), so the reflex and the survival step ask again the moment the bucket pours. The hold asks again at four more health or half what the health was, whichever is less, so it never runs to the death ("Asked again at the next hurt of the burning" under two). burn_out says the fire left as health against the health ("about 11 seconds of fire left at a health a second that armour does not stop is about 11 health, and the bot has 8.3: it dies of the burning in about 8.3 seconds, before the fire ends, unless something puts it out first. Asked again at about 4.3 health (4 more), or when another way to put it out comes"); douse_bucket says the fire is out about half a second from now (bucket to hand, look, pour; not measured), a hurt at most meanwhile, "where burning on takes all 8.3 health the bot has, its death in about 8.3 seconds", then the water back, about a second in all; the condition says the death and when. Every other gameplay question asked while the body is alight carries state.alight (body.js burningSays): the fire left, the health it takes, the death if it is one, and a held way out with when it is asked again. Tests (test/burning-body.test.js, the ground from the saved region file): the step at 06:09:17 offers the bucket and pours onto (135, 14, 100); the fall into the dry cell at 06:09:09 offers it; burning out held as the one way in the air ends on landing and the fire reflex returns; the 8.3 question's texts and the hold at half under eight; survival_priority says the burning and the hold, and nothing when not alight. All six fail without the change. Replay cases body-alight-cave-8.3-bucket-mid-244-bb and body-alight-cave-0.3-bucket-mid-244-bb, the recorded states with the new text: probed ten times each, the recorded text burn_out 10 of 10 in both, the new douse_bucket 10 of 10 in both; 5 of 5 each in the suite. npm test 1929 of 1929, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 55 of 57 with the two known gaps (blaze-at-1.4-cover-missing, fortress-approach-mine-then-cross-242ab). Still open: the scoop that walked the bot into the pool (fill_bucket's scoop at the lava's edge) is not looked at here; the bucket's half second is the mechanics, not a measurement; pouring into a cell beside lava turns that lava to stone or obsidian, not said; none of this has run live.
597. *mid-242-ab-nether-3-fortress-1 (25592) again, "wait in shelter" 16 of the 19 minutes to 06:45 and turn_priority survival 26 times of 27: a second pocket, not 589's, sealed twelve blocks from a blaze spawner, and the one way a player would take out of it never offered.* It left 589's pocket at 05:42 (leave, 0.54), went back toward the portal for food (leave_nether go_back), set the rods aside at 06:10 and took them up again at 06:19, and walked the fortress's piers south of 589's pocket: dig_down at 06:23:53 (left at once), seal at 06:26:58 at (-289, 49, -176) and tunnel_out chosen from it at 06:27:16 (offered there), leave_nether go_back again at 06:27:43 (0.35), then take_cover and seal (0.32) at 06:28:18 at (-275, 56, -174). From 06:28:26 to 07:05 and on, pocket_next stay 63 times (0.10 to 0.53, none_good 0.18 to 0.29), 12.8 health, hunger 11, nothing to eat. What it waits in: the region file (as saved at 06:48Z) has the pocket on the lip of a nether-brick pier that stands in the air over the fortress's lower decks, its seal two netherrack blocks; and a blaze spawner at (-281, 63, -167), 11.6 blocks off and 7 above, within the 16 at which it makes more. What from: the blaze it sealed against (9 blocks off, never in sight), gone within minutes; after that the spawner's own. The blazes at 9 to 11 blocks are real: the threats list from the entity tracker at 06:46:54 has a skeleton at 5.9 and blazes at 8.6, 9.3, 10.2, 10.6 and 11.4, none in sight, and blazes within their 48: 6 at 06:28:26, 18 at 06:38:22, 19 at 06:46:54; the saved wait record at 06:56 had 27 mobs within 24. What the options said: at 06:28:26 leave went on with the trip back (589's hold); at 06:37:43, still sealed, the ten-minute hold lapsed, and from 06:38:22 leave went "back to the obtain blaze rods step" with go_for_food/return_for_food beside it (0.05). Stay said the minutes, the mobs each "come from 13 to 9 blocks off, never with the bot in sight", no daylight, no health without food, and "Neither daylight nor health comes to this wait: only the mobs outside moving off would change it", beside a spawner that makes more of them while the bot stays: the mobs outside were not going to move off, and their count was said nowhere. Is staying right among what was offered? Defensible: out by a door at 12.8 health, not healing, past five blazes within 11 and nineteen within 48, two fireballs that land end it; Jev's none_good at a fifth to a quarter said the move was missing. The move missing: tunnel_out. passageOut surveyed one way only, level along the axis away from the spawner: north through the pier, 7 cells to z -181, where the floor is air, ending 16.97 from the spawner, short of 17 (and measured across, not in three dimensions); east, 4 cells to the air at x -271; the other two not looked at. The pier is solid nether brick below the pocket from y 48 to 55. Now (survival.js passageOut): each way that does not lead toward the keeper is surveyed, the one straight away first and then the two across, level first and then a stair down, a block lower a step with three blocks dug a step, and the first that ends clear is the passage, its cells kept and dug as surveyed (tunnelOut follows them). A spawner's clearance is measured in three dimensions, as its sixteen is. Here: north, a stair of 4 steps to (-275, 52, -178), 12 blocks dug, about 15 seconds, 17 from the spawner. The option says the stair ("a stair down a block each step to 4 below the pocket's floor (12 blocks dug)"), and with blazes about that the rock stands round it and the wall toward them stays; and it, the blaze passage and the leave go on from its end with the trip back for food while that is held. The hold: the minutes sealed in a pocket sealed after it was chosen are not the trip's (netherLeaveHeld's sealedAt, the wait's start), and leaving or tunnelling out to go on with it is choosing it again (its clock starts over). pocket-wait.js: with a spawner in reach, the stay says "Neither daylight nor health comes to this wait, and the mobs outside do not move off: the blaze spawner 12 blocks off makes more of them while the bot is within its 16 blocks" (state pocketSoFar.waitingFor), and stay and leave say the count within 24 at the wait's first look and now when it has changed ("Within 24 blocks of the pocket: 5 mobs at the wait's first look 18 minutes ago, 20 now"; pocketSoFar.mobsWithin24). Stay and leave stay Jev's. Probed against the live model on the recorded askings rebuilt through the code (survival.step in the pier's blocks from the region file, the trial's saved survival state and goal, the recorded health, hunger, inventory and threats at their distances, more blazes to the recorded count within 48, the hold as chosen at 06:27:43), 5 runs each. 06:46:54 (19 minutes): main's code stay 5 of 5 (0.50 to 0.59, as recorded 0.50); now tunnel_out 5 of 5 (0.52 to 0.58, stay 0.20 to 0.23). 06:38:22 (10 minutes, the hold just lapsed on main): main stay 5 of 5 (0.57 to 0.64); now tunnel_out 5 of 5 (0.42 to 0.48). The new text without tunnel_out: stay 5 of 5 still (0.43 to 0.46), leave up from 0.08 to 0.30: the passage is what moves it. Added to the replay suite as pocket-spawner-pier-no-food-tunnel-25592 (tunnel_out 5 of 5), the pier's blocks as test/fixtures/spawner-pier-mid-242-ab.json. Why it keeps ending up sealed in: its way back to the portal and its fortress search keep walking it within sixteen of that spawner, where the blazes it came for are, and each encounter there answers take_cover then seal ("within 16 blocks of here in the last 15 minutes the bot has been sealed in a pocket with mobs watching 12 times"); that is the stance's question (agent 596's), not changed here. Tests (test/pocket-wait.test.js): the trial's pocket in the region file's blocks, five blazes at the first look and fifteen more over nineteen minutes, the trip chosen 35 seconds before the seal: tunnel_out north, a 4-step stair to 4 below, 12 blocks, 15 seconds, 17 from the spawner, going on with the trip from its end; the stay says the mobs do not move off with the spawner making more and the count from 5 to 2x; the leave goes on with the trip at 19 minutes; chosen, the passage is dug and walked cell by cell to (-275, 52, -178), beyond the spawner's sixteen, and the trip's clock starts over. A trip chosen eleven minutes before the seal has lapsed: the leave goes to the rung, return_for_food is offered, the passage goes back to the rung. 589's test now keeps the trip at 25 minutes sealed and lapses it for one chosen eleven minutes before; survival.test.js's wet passage is wet on every way, and water behind the west run only gives a level passage north. Each fails without the change. npm test 1939 of 1939, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 58 of 60 with the two known gaps. Still open: the passage ends inside the pier, and the walk from there to the portal 344 blocks off is the pathfinder's, not looked at here; a stair up, or a turn partway, is not surveyed; the spawner's mob is said as "mob" where the server sent no block entity; the stance that seals the bot in by that spawner again and again is 596's; none of this has run live.

598. *The audit's measures for the deploy of 2026-09-28T06:45Z: waiting, the stance asked while nothing changes, none-good runs, and before against after (notes 558, 573).* `progress-audit.js` now says per trial over the window, in the text and in `--json` (`review.waits`, `review.stance`, `review.noneGoodStreak`, and their `targets` lines `waitShare`, `stanceRate`, `noneGoodStreak`), each flagged when its target is missed: the clocked minutes spent waiting, as a share (under 20%): hold_bunker (the step), wait_in_shelter (the pocket_next stay and every wait in a shelter), pillar_hold (a pillar top), back_to_wall, wait_for_day(_sealed), and a stay_in_fortress walk (a fortress_leg answer) for its three minutes or until the legs are asked again, the find_fortress minutes inside it; encounter_stance and turn_priority askings a minute over the stretches of a minute or more in which health stayed within 1 and the nearest mob's distance within 2 blocks of the stretch's start (a mob coming or going, or a change of dimension, starts a new stretch; health is in every frame, the mobs only in an event's full frame, so the nearest mob is what the last one said), under 1; and the longest run of none_good answers in a row to one question, other questions between not breaking it (no target: said, not flagged). The run clock is now read from every frame that carries it, joined, not the last frame's half hour alone, so a window or a trial longer than thirty minutes is clocked in full; with no clock the observations' current survival action counts before their step. `--since <iso>` keeps the trials begun after it. `--cohort <iso> [--since <iso>] [--json]` reads every trial record (artifacts/midgame) over its whole span (to a done verdict's end; a verdict not done says only the minutes at its last update, so the last write to its port's flight files before the next trial there ends it) and sums before and after the time: reaskAfterHold (the bot log read forward in chunks, half a gigabyte for mid-243-ab, each line on the side of its newest stamp), quickNothing (and its busiest question a 15 min against the target of 5), stallShare, waitShare, the stance rate, the none-good share and longest run, deaths per trial hour by cause from the servers' own logs (death-index.js's pattern; the day from the archive's name carried past midnight, or latest.log's last change counted back, and a day wrong by one moved into its world's trial), and by the side a trial began on the minutes to the Nether (trials not begun at a checkpoint), Nether to the fortress (not begun at a fortress checkpoint) and to the first rod, and from a fortress checkpoint to the first rod. A trial running across the time counts on both sides by time. 458 trials in 25 seconds.
