First run, the thirteen live trials at 07:03 (window 15 min): waiting over on mid-242-ab-nether-3-fortress-1 (100%, 13.9 min in a shelter, pocket_next stay 29 of 29), mid-242-af-nether-3 (74%) and mid-242-af-nether-2 (42%); the stance over on mid-208-k-nether-4-fortress-1 at 7.68 a minute (115 askings in 15 unchanged minutes, encounter_stance fight 98 times), and on four more at 1.15 to 1.56; the longest none-good run 8 stillness_detour on mid-243-af-nether-3. `--cohort 2026-09-28T06:45Z` at about 07:12, before (458 trials, 520.8 trial hours, 334.9 clocked in the 355 with a flight record) and after (the 13 running across it, 4.5 hours, the first 27 minutes): reaskAfterHold 1,927 (3.7 an hour) and 72 (16.1 an hour); quickNothing 45.6 and 24.5 a 15 min, the busiest question survival_priority 16.8 and encounter_stance 11.7; stallShare 4.2% and 11.1%; waitShare 9.1% (wait in shelter 1,421 min, pillar hold 227, stay_in_fortress 115, hold_bunker 50, back_to_wall 16) and 15%; the stance while unchanged 0.64 and 1.6 a minute; none good 5.3% and 11.7%, longest runs 1,393 encounter_stance in a row (mid-244-ad-nether-2) and 12 stillness_detour; deaths 0.349 and 0.224 an hour (182 and 1; before, zombie 26, creeper 21, lava 19; after, one lava); to the Nether 75 of 339 (median 56 min), Nether to fortress 30 of 134 (median 12), Nether to first rod 1 of 134, fortress checkpoint to first rod 1 of 57, and none of the four after: no trial has begun since. What the records cannot give: the minutes-to measures after the deploy until trials begin after it; the fortress sighting across trials is the midgame verdict's fortress milestone, not the sighting (the landmark's firstAt lives in the port's world file, which the next trial overwrites); the first rod only from a flight record, since the trial record holds the count and not the time; the flight record runs from 2026-09-27T01:28Z on most ports, so 103 earlier trials have no flight measures (their hours and deaths still count); the nearest mob between events is carried from the last full frame. Tests (progress-audit.test.js): a Nether window with a bunker held, a shelter, a pillar top and a stay_in_fortress walk cut short (9 of 20 min, flagged), the stance asked 18 times in six still minutes (health at 19.5 within 1) and twice after the zombie moved off (1 a minute, flagged), and unstuck_move none_good seven times running between other questions; a mob closing two blocks every 40 s counts no still stretch, a health drop splits two; deaths across midnight, latest.log counted back, a wrong day moved into its trial; a bot log scanned in 64-byte chunks split at the deploy; and the cohort over two trial records either side of a deploy, their deaths from a server log, the first's Nether and fortress minutes, and `--since`. npm test 1942 of 1942.

596. *mid-208-k-nether-4-fortress-1 (25589) again, still running at 06:48: the audit at 06:45 with "fight" the top step 17 of 20 minutes, encounter_stance asked 499 times in 20 minutes (467 the same answer), none good 365 of 525, one new column of ground.* Read from the bot log, the flight records and a copy of the saved region. At 06:27 the bot came down off note 590's pillar at (-38.5, 46, 60.3) and walked five blocks along a one-wide ledge at y 46 to its end, a netherrack stub two long at (-39, 45, 65 and 66) over a four-block drop; the hoglin stood below on the nylium, at y 43 and then (-38.9, 44, 67.9) on the corner of a block, two to three blocks down and two across. It never came nearer, never struck (7.9 health throughout, hunger 16 to 17, nothing to eat) and never left. Two loops, one cause. From 06:27:20 to 06:29:42, 350 askings in two and a half minutes, every 0.2 to 0.3 seconds: fight each time (none good 0.45 to 0.57), each run at the hoglin finding no way at once (the charge's route fails, it is set aside as unreachable, the stance fails), said to the next asking as "Tried 6 times in the last 3 seconds here, and it failed", no why, and offered again. From 06:29:42 at the ledge's end, the hoglin 2.98 blocks off: fight every 7.4 seconds for twenty minutes (fight 0.34 to 0.57, none good 0.16 to 0.26). The fight's run counted the hoglin as in reach by distance (3.2 or under) and went to the swing, but the swing's own test (canStrike) had no line: the eye at 47.6, the ray to the hoglin's middle cut by the top of the ledge's last block; so the fight "swung" a second at a time with nothing swung, held until its estimate's 5.4 seconds ran out, and was asked again with the same facts. Why Jev said none of these: every stance opened "at its own speed ... it can be at arm's length in about 0.4 seconds" and priced the fight 8.1 damage from 7.9 health, the pillar and the pocket 21.9, the retreat no way out. The hoglin was counted as getting to the bot because walk-reach (note 525) began its search, for every walker, from the cells within a creeper's three and a half blocks of the bot as well as those a walker touches it from: a creeper lights through a floor, a hoglin does not. The cell under the ledge, (-40, 43, 65), 3.2 from the bot and three below, was such a cell, so a hoglin that could stand there "got to" the bot. Replayed on the saved ground: the hoglin reaches as before; with the lights for creepers alone, it has no way within twelve blocks (any way round 21 blocks or more), at the 06:12 pillar, the 06:27 burst and the ledge's end alike. The move missing, as a player makes it: step one block to the ledge's end and strike down. From (-39, 46, 66) the eye clears the edge, the hoglin's box is 2.3 blocks off, and its top (45.4) is 0.6 under the feet: a mob's blow lands where its box widened sideways, not up, touches the target (26.1.2 Mob.isWithinMeleeAttackRange, note 586), so it cannot strike back; seven swings of the iron sword kill it, and it drops two to four raw porkchops, the food the bot lacked. Now: walk-reach searches for a creeper's reach only for creepers, and with creepers and other walkers together searches each by its own (walk-reach.js). A new stance, strike_from_above (src/strike-below.js, survival.js strikeBelowOption): offered for the nearest walker within six, not a creeper, whose top is at or below the feet of a stand on the bot's own level within two steps from which the swing's own test (canStrike, now with `from`) reaches it; said with the step, the rule, the swings, its way up (walk-reach's: none, round, or one it can walk and then it is the fight), the knockback (a hoglin barely moves), what it drops and where (its floor below, fetched by going down) and that nothing to eat is carried, and the price with every other mob still reaching; run: the step (no towers, digs or drops), crouched, swings while it is below and in reach, failed with why when none could be made. The fight's run swings only when something can be struck; otherwise the charge and the stand as before, each failure now saying why ("nothing was struck: the hoglin 2.98 blocks off is 2 blocks below the feet and out of the sword's reach from where the bot stands (no clear swing at it), and the run at it found no way to it", or "held 8 seconds facing ..."). And the general rule in the stance loop (stanceStep): whether a stance acted is measured from when it was chosen (a swing, a block placed or dug, a step of a block, what is carried changed; stanceMark, stanceActed). A stance that failed without acting, or a striking stance (fight, fight_from_footing, rail_and_fight, strike_from_above) held to its end without acting, is kept with the scene it ended in (the bot's place, health, each mob about); while the scene is the same (the bot within a block, each mob within a block of where it stood and none come, health within a point) it is left out of the question and said in the state (notOfferedNow: "ended here without acting, the last 1 second ago: ...; nothing has changed here since ..., so it would end the same; offered again when something changes"), only while two or more other ways stay on offer: with fewer, leaving it out would be the code's choice taken unasked, and it stays on offer with that said. A meal cut short is not counted (note 475). Asked faster than anything changed, each answer ending at once, is the loop; the question is now asked again with the same way on offer only when something has changed. Tests (test/strike-below.test.js, on the saved ground, test/fixtures/ledge-hoglin-25589.json): the hoglin two below has no way to the bot, and a creeper there still lights; from the ledge's end a block on the sword reaches it (not from where the bot stood) and it cannot reach up; the recorded 06:47:06 question offers strike_from_above with its words, the fight says none of them can get to the bot, and the run steps to (-39, 46, 66) and strikes the hoglin; the 06:27:21 fight whose run finds no way fails at once with its why, is left out at the next asking and said in notOfferedNow, and is offered again once the hoglin walks a block and a half; a minute of askings at the ledge's end chooses the fight once, not eight times, and strike_from_above is on offer. Each fails without the change (the last hangs on the old fight's second of swinging at nothing under the test's clock, and is timed). Changed tests: survival.test.js note 521's pillar knocked off now places its blocks (a stance that acted and failed stays on offer, said); pillar-wait.test.js's recorded hold chooses the fight once, then finds it left out with "held 15 seconds: nothing was struck" (was ten times, each said). Probed against the live model, 5 each: the 06:47:06 question as recorded, fight 5 of 5 (0.35 to 0.42, none good 0.22 to 0.26); as now asked (hold_on_span, fight, strike_from_above, keep_working, retreat; the recorded clocks, positions and death's cost carried over), strike_from_above 5 of 5 (0.62 to 0.67, fight 0.16 to 0.18, none good 0.00); added to the replay suite as ledge-hoglin-below-strike-from-above-208k. npm test 1942 of 1942, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 58 of 60 with the two known gaps. Live, with the walk-reach fix the hoglin under the ledge is no threat at all (immediateThreat leaves out a walker with no way to the bot), so the encounter ends and the work goes on; strike_from_above is offered when the stance is asked with such a mob about. Still open: the food the hoglin is goes unasked when it is no threat (the food question does not offer striking from above; a hunt's walk to it has no route); the drops fall to its floor below and fetching them is a separate walk down; the pillar hold of 06:12 to 06:26 (pillar every fifteen seconds with nothing changed) is a wait chosen and is still asked at its estimate's end; none of this has run live.

599. *Busy but not progressing, in the layer note 571's ledger left out (a design review's advice, and note 598's audit at 07:12: reaskAfterHold 16 an hour against 0, the stance asked 1.6 a minute while nothing changed against under 1, 7.7 on mid-208-k-nether-4-fortress-1, and a none-good run of 1,393).* Note 571 built one ledger with rests, escalation to a parent and the rung's ten-minute budget, and exempted the layer where the day's loops live: SAY_ONLY and NEVER_HELD kept encounter_stance, body_way, shield_policy, turn_priority and stillness_detour out of resting and escalation, every wait was recorded as `waited` and never came to nothing, and watchRung counted no minute a wait held (permittedWait), so a pocket (notes 584, 589, 597), a pillar (590) or a stance loop (596) could run half an hour with nothing above it asking. Each hold then grew a ledger of its own (pocket-wait, pillar-wait, the stand's results). Now, in five parts. (1) A wait is a ledger entry with an outcome (tried.js). A wait begun (an answer given while a hold or an emergency holds the turn, as before, and now also any answer that is a wait by what it is: stay, back_to_wall, the dug-in and spawner stands, stay_in_fortress, wait_at_spawner, wait_for_day_sealed, rest_to_heal, pillar, seal, dig_down, hold_on_span, take_cover, out_of_sight, nook) keeps the scene it began in: health, hunger, the bot's place, the nearest mob within sixteen (which, how far, in sight), how many are in sight, the last swing. Settled, a wait of ten seconds or more whose scene did not change (health and hunger within a point, moved under two blocks, the nearest no nearer or farther by four and not come or gone, the same number in sight, no swing) came to nothing: blocked, said as a wait ("Held from about here 2 times in the last 3 minutes, 3 minutes in all, and nothing changed in any of them: health 12.8, nothing within 16 blocks, no swing throughout"), resting and escalating as a way does; one whose world changed is `waited`, with what changed; a wait that failed is blocked with its error. The ledger reads these back on every question, a stance's too (a say-only question reads only its waits, never its failures, which its own options say, note 521). A resting wait is left out only while two or more other ways stay on offer (note 596's rule); with fewer it stays on offer with its rest said; a say-only question is never escalated from it. The three hold records keep their texts: pocket-wait's and pillar-wait's mob records now also feed part 5; the stand results are unchanged, the stand's answer itself being the ledger's wait. (2) The rung's budget runs on the wall clock whoever holds the turn (arbiter.js rungWatch, called at the head of every pass in place of the work's own call): only a wait something is bringing to an end is not counted (stillness.js waitEnds: asleep, with the player, a batch cooking, health coming back on a recover step or rest_to_heal, and the Overworld night in a shelter); a pocket, a pillar's top, a stand or a fight is ten minutes like any other. Due, the rung's question is raised for the work as before and says which layer has had the turn and for how long. (3) None good as escalation (decisions/index.js; repeats.js noneGoodAfter and noneGoodSpent): two answers in a row to one question with none good the top at 0.4 or more, to the same situation from within four blocks, mark the question spent there for five minutes. The situation is the fingerprint of the facts less the record of the answers themselves (failedHereJustNow, notOfferedNow, previousStance, the ledger's and the repeat rule's words, the trail, the clock): on mid-244-ad-nether-2 those were all that changed between 1,708 of its 1,772 sure none-good stance askings. At the second, the question's parent is told through the path a resting way takes (tried.escalate, whatFailedBelow), the answer above that led there is marked blocked, and the stall is raised, not thrown: the best listed is carried out meanwhile. While spent, the question is not asked: the best of the options now offered by that answer's weights is taken, said once ("[spent]"). (4) A hold ends on divergence, not on the clock (src/holds.js). A stance now carries what it was chosen on: its estimate (`expects`), each mob it was chosen against (id, distance, in sight), and the options on offer (those left out by note 596 included). At the end of its estimate's time, a stance with nothing new is held on without a question, 15, 30, then 60 seconds at a time, and every look while it is held on checks what it was chosen on: more damage than priced at its own rate plus one blow, a mob it was chosen against nearer by four, into or out of sight, gone or hurting the bot, a shot on its way, the bot off the spot it held (1.5 blocks, taken at the first holding-on), or a way not on offer when it was chosen (body.js's held way out of the fire, note 595, made general); any of these asks again, said in previousStance.askedAgainFor. At five minutes it is asked again ("held 5 minutes and nothing it was chosen on changed") and the rung's question is due at once (tried.rungDue). The physical triggers are as they were (six health, a newcomer within six, a shooter's line where it hid, a creeper's line, a hit while leaving them be), and within the estimate's own time only they and the estimate apply; keep_working, a meal and a striking stance that has not acted (note 596) are not held on; the reflexes give way to a stance held on for its hold's time (danger.js stanceHeld, stanceMobs). (5) The ways off priced as the hold is (src/held-off.js): every look the stance and the pocket take records each mob about, and one about three minutes or more that in the last three has not come nearer by two, come into sight or hurt the bot (or, from a pocket's or a pillar's own record, never nearer and never seen) is priced at what it has done, nothing, on every option that does not go at it, the hold and each way off alike (stanceCost, the fight's shots while it stands with nothing to swing at, the work's shots, the pocket's leave); a fight that goes at it is priced as that fight; each option says so ("Held off: the piglin 13 blocks off (5 minutes, out of sight; about 8 a hit should it come). It has been about that long without coming nearer, coming into sight or hurting the bot, so it is priced at what it has done, nothing, on every option here that does not go at it, the hold and the ways off alike; a fight that goes at it is priced as that fight."). This replaces note 584's "should they all come at the bot at once" where it applies. Measured by replaying each trial's recorded decisions from the flight record, in time order, through the new code's own functions (holds.diverged and extend, repeats.situation, noneGoodAfter and noneGoodSpent, tried.sceneChanges), the recorded mobs matched by kind and nearest distance; it counts the askings that would no longer happen, not what the bot would then have done. 25589 mid-208-k-nether-4-fortress-1: 1,305 askings to 492, encounter_stance 1,053 to 240 (567 held on, 246 spent by none good); its pillar 05:24 to 05:53 125 to 7, its pillar 06:12 to 06:26 59 to 3, the burst 06:27:20 to 06:29:42 340 to 102, the ledge 06:29:42 to 06:50 188 to 39, and 06:52 to 07:12 (the audit's 7.7 a minute) 87 to 25; four stretches of 21 to 37 minutes with the survival layer asking and no rod, each now asked the rung's question two or three times. 25598 mid-242-af: encounter_stance 111 to 68, its pillar 05:14:51 to 05:26:58 46 to 8, and the rung's question once. 25592 mid-242-ab-nether-3-fortress-1: 111 of its 129 pocket_next askings were two pockets (05:09, 33 minutes, 39 askings; 06:28, 47 minutes, 72); the stay would rest 4.2 and 3.1 minutes in, left out with two or more other ways, after which 91 askings of the stay would not have come, and the rung's question comes at ten minutes in each; encounter_stance 81 to 66. mid-244-ad-nether-2 (25590): 1,925 askings to 245, encounter_stance 1,800 to 120, the none-good runs of 1,392 and 380 each ended at their second. Probed against the live model, 5 runs each: 25598 at 05:19:42, the pillar's first cap, as recorded pillar 5 of 5 (0.61 to 0.71); as now asked (askedAgainFor the cap, the pillar's wait said from the ledger, the piglin 8 off in sight, the hoglin and the four unseen priced at what they had done, the prices re-worked with the code's estimate: the pillar 48.4 to 23.3, the fight's shots 100.5 to 50.3, the work's 101 to 50) pillar 5 of 5 (0.56 to 0.69); at the second cap, 05:24:31, the pillar resting and left out, retreat 4 and fight 1 (retreat 0.20 to 0.30, fight 0.22 to 0.27, none good 0.21 to 0.25), and that recorded retreat says a run fails at once (the recorded texts are from before note 590, with no way down on offer). 25592 at 05:12:13, the pocket as recorded stay 5 of 5 (0.52 to 0.58); with the first stay that came to nothing said on it, stay 5 of 5 (0.50 to 0.54); at 05:13:05, the stay resting and left out, leave 5 of 5. So the facts alone did not move Jev off a quiet hold in either; the rest, the cap and the rung's question are what end it. Not added to the replay suite: the pillar's answer once rested is a way that fails at once, not one to hold Jev to. Tests (test/waits.test.js): two pocket stays with nothing changed rest the stay, left out with three ways on offer, and kept with its rest said with one; the scene's changes; a stance held to nothing twice rests (the pillar left out, no escalation); a hunt's stand held twice with nothing changed rests; the rung's question after ten minutes sealed in a pocket with the survival layer holding the turn every pass; waitEnds; none good sure twice to the same situation (the failures just now changing) asks twice in six and takes the fight, escalates to survival_priority raised and not thrown, a new situation is asked, an unsure none good starts no run; fortress_leg spent escalates to the rung; holds.diverged and extend; held-off's quiet and the prices with and without it; and, as a guard, a resting wait left out only for two open ways, every way resting still escalating. test/pillar-wait.test.js: the recorded hold of note 590 is asked at 15, 315, 330 and 630 seconds (was 45 times), the zombie's blow asks again at 60 seconds, coming down the pillar asks again, and a hoglin gone out of sight and back asks at once each time. test/pocket-wait.test.js: the piglin held off five minutes is priced at nothing on the leave and the stay. Each but the guard fails on the code before the change. npm test 1961 of 1961, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 59 of 61 with the two known gaps. Still open: the way down from a pillar prices walkers at what they did while the bot was out of their reach, which is not what they do once it is within it (each one's hit is said beside it); turn_priority is still asked each minute while a hold stands; a spent stance escalates to survival_priority, which is asked only at night or for food, so there the escalation is said, not asked sooner; the replays do not measure reaskAfterHold, which counts note 560's repeat holds, unchanged here; within a stance's first estimate only the old triggers apply, and sight flickering at an edge can end a hold held on; none of this has run live.
600. *The bot's own dig or step into the lava sea, six times on 2026-09-28 from the fortress stage saves (main at 87a6bdd, notes 571 to 599 live), and the rods set aside a minute into a trial and taken back in the same second.* The deaths, each read from its flight frames and its death snapshot's region file. mid-242-af-fortress-1 (25583, 11:37:41): restock_blocks' crouched walk to its drop (walk_to_drop) left the body on the corner of a cobblestone at (-105, 40, 105), and as the work went on it fell three blocks to the ledge at y 38. The unstuck question was asked at 11:37:39.6 with the body still falling (y 40.8, then 39.4), its feet read as (-104, 40, 104): every move was a move from a cell the body was passing through. step_south (0.34) said "dropping 1. one block past it, a drop of 7 blocks under it: falling 7 blocks costs about 4 health"; the seven blocks ended in the lava sea, and nothing said so (dropBelow stopped at the first block that was not open and called it ground). Run from where the body landed, the step pressed forward against the netherrack at (-104, 38, 105), turned a little east by the look taken mid-fall; the body slid east along the wall into (-103, 38, 104), open to the sea, and fell: 19.6 to nothing. So the step put it in the lava, not restock's walk, and nothing in the option or its run knew the lava was there: the option read the cell past it and not where the fall ended, the landing was not read with standsInLava, and the run held forward for up to 2.5 seconds with nothing keeping it to the two cells of the step. Reproduced under prismarine-physics on the saved region: without a guard the body ends at (-102.2, 28.6, 106.4) in the lava, the recorded (-102.3, 29.3, 106.3). mid-242-ah-nether-2-fortress-2 (25592, 11:54:13): on its own dirt span at (-168, 47, 26), gravel the only block carried and no pickaxe, Jev answered none_good to unstuck_move five times; the fallback took dig_down, dig_down, step_north, dig_down and then place_east, "Put a gravel into the space east, at the feet: a step up", into a cell with fifteen blocks of air under it to the sea. The gravel fell at once. climb_east was offered 0.8 seconds later onto the gravel read before it went (0.57), the body jumped into the empty cell and fell, the next question was again asked mid-fall at y 39.7, and the body was in the lava at 32.4. mid-243-af-fortress-2 (25600, 11:55:21): the stall's blind step (skills.js shakeLoose, stage 1) stepped a block south onto a ledge thirty-eight blocks over the sea and, released once it had moved a block, ran on off it (mine_nearby was answered mid-fall). mid-242-ae-nether-2-fortress-3 (25600, 11:58:02) and mid-243-ag-fortress-2 (25592, 12:06:07), the same both times: the mine's and the restock's crouched walk to a drop stopped with the body's middle over the sea, held up by the edge of the block beside by a hundredth of a block, and when the walk let the crouch go, what was left of its speed took it off (x -102.713 to -102.700, straight down 25 blocks; x -144.71 to -144.70, 24 blocks). Not the tunnel and not lava flowing in: in neither was a block dug under or beside the body. Now, one check for every dig and one for every upright step. terrain.js fallFrom reads a fall to its end, at any depth, and a landing where the body stands in lava is lava (note 580). terrain.js digExposes is the dig's: a block the body stands on or beside (its columns and the ring round them, from the floor to over the head) is not dug with lava in any of its six neighbors, source or flowing (lava beside or over it flows in, lava under it opens a hole onto the lava beside the feet); the floor under the body is not dug out, nothing else holding the body, where the fall under it ends in lava; and no block is dug beside the lowest of a column of gravel or sand the body stands on where it drops into lava or for the body's health (note 592). It is checked on bot.dig itself (skills.js digGuardPlugin, loaded with the other plugins in session.js), so every dig, the mine's, the restock's, the tunnel's, the bunker's, the bridge's, the unstuck move's and the pathfinder's own, meets it and is refused with why ("Not dug: the netherrack at (-104, 37, 104): the floor under the body at (-104, 37, 104) dug out, it falls 6 blocks into lava"). The step's is motion.js move, the one way the bot holds keys: an upright move reads the whole fall ahead into lava, not four blocks (burningAhead deep), and refuses it; a crouched move keeps the four-block look (the edge holds the body, so a span over the sea is still walked crouched to its end), and does not end with the body's middle over a fall into lava: it walks back onto the block it hangs from, still crouched, before the crouch lets go (backOnFooting). motion.within keeps a move to its own cells, stopped where the body's middle leaves them. The blind step goes through move (it held its keys by hand), keeps to its two cells, and is not taken toward a ledge where a step that runs on past it falls into lava. The unstuck moves (unstuck.js): read once the body has landed (landed, up to two seconds); a move read from another cell is not run; no move ends with the body in lava (standsInLava), no dig down whose fall ends in lava, no dig that digExposes refuses, no move that drops the floor stood on (floorDrops), no move onto gravel or sand resting on nothing over lava, and no gravel or sand put where nothing under it holds it (another carried block is used there if there is one); each said in here.notOffered. A drop past a step that ends in lava is said as that, with what one touch costs this body (lavaTouchSays); a level step with lava past it is crouched; the run keeps to the step's two cells. The recovery adviser's relocations are not offered onto footing where the body stands in lava. Which move the five none_good answers on 25592 missed is not settled here: the bot carried no pickaxe, so no netherrack was offered to dig, and its moves were a block at a time on its own span; a way back along the span to ground it had stood on, several cells long, is the likeliest, and nothing offers a walk of more than one cell. The rung's question. At 11:37:26 on 25583 it was not the ten-minute budget. The stage save mid-242-af-053454 held the rung's budget at 22 seconds of idle; note 583's shift by the gap was applied; note 599's none-good escalation fired nowhere in either log. From 11:33:56, 58 seconds into the trial, to 11:37:26 the rung's question was asked 22 times, every one an escalation: the legs, restock_blocks and return_for_blocks each came back at once (0 blocks carried, the portal unreachable), note 570's streak held them, fortress_leg's every way rested five minutes, and each pass asked fortress_leg again, escalated, and asked the rung's question again, the rung's own answers, each met at once by the same escalation, coming back at once in their turn (keep_at_it "10 times, 10 coming to nothing" by 11:34:43), until setting the rods aside was the answer taken. Its first asking told Jev "worked on this rung in 31 minutes on it: 48 answers given": thirty of those minutes and all but a few of the answers were from before the save (the rung's `since`, moved by the gap, counted from 05:04). The rods were set aside 8 times in those 3.5 minutes and taken back 6 times, each within a second: twice by the stall's take_up_obtain_blaze_rods ("set aside 1 minutes ago", the minutes floored up to one) and four times by leave_nether's search_on, the rods' own question, which offered them straight back. After 11:35:09 the rods waited (rods_waiting) and the rung in hand by its clock was still the rods: the stall's escalations brought the rung's question for a rung already set aside ("keep at the none"), and at 11:37:26 it set them aside again, restarting their half hour. mid-242-af-nether-2-fortress-1 (25584) was the same: fortress_leg's quick streak held at 11:35:45, the rung's question 11 times in the next 75 seconds, the rods set aside and taken back by search_on four times, each within three seconds, then brought to the rung's question and set aside again at 11:40:00 and 11:42:33 while they waited (the watcher's loop of "The blaze rods step waits"). Now: a save that lay unplayed longer than the rung's own budget is a new session on the rung (tried.resumed): its budget and its count start at the resume, and the minutes before the save are said beside them ("in 0.9 minutes on it in this session (and 30 minutes before the save it was taken up from)"). A rung set aside is not the rung in hand while it rests (tried.rungOf): its budget does not run, its question is not asked (the stall's is), and set_aside_rung is not offered for it. An escalation for every way resting says when the first comes off rest (escalateFrom's until, from tried.read); the rung's question then offers until_rest_ends, other work until then with the rung kept in hand and not set aside, and keeping at it says that from here the next pass meets the same rests and the question comes again. Setting a rung aside keeps what it was for, from where and until when (goal.rungAside: the rests below, or five minutes); while that stands from within four blocks of there, taking it up again is not offered, neither the stall's take_up nor leave_nether's search_on, and is said why (takeUpNotOffered, searchOnNotOffered); offered, take_up says how long ago in seconds or minutes, from where and for what. Tests: test/unstuck-lava.test.js on the region 25583's snapshot saved at 11:37:11 (x -110 to -98, y 29 to 43, z 99 to 114, the restock's dig taken out; the unstuck options read from it are the recorded ones word for word, test/fixtures/unstuck-lava-mid-242-af.json): the step south says the drop into lava and what a touch costs; the step read at y 40 is not run with the body at 38; read once landed, no step south is offered; the recorded step run from where it landed, under prismarine-physics, stops at the edge of its cells with no tick in the lava; a step onto soul sand level with a lava source is not offered; on note 592's gravel beach no pillar; no dig down whose fall ends in lava; on 25592's span (test/fixtures/unstuck-span-mid-242-ah.json, its snapshot of 11:53:29) no gravel put east and no climb onto the gravel read before it fell. test/dig-step-lava.test.js: the dig guard refuses the floor under the body on 25583's ledge, a dig beside the body with lava beside it, and a dig beside the beach's gravel, and lets other digs through; an upright step over the fall into lava is refused at any depth (the recorded heading on the ledge, under physics, stays on it); a crouched move keeps the four-block look; the blind step does not step toward a ledge that runs into lava; and on the region 25600's mid-242-ae-nether-2-fortress-3 snapshot saved at 11:57:37 (test/fixtures/drop-edge-mid-242-ae.json), the crouched walk east to the edge over the sea ends back on its footing, where without it the body falls into the lava at y 28.4. test/rung-aside.test.js: with every way below resting the rung's question offers until_rest_ends and says keeping at it meets the same rests, and the set-aside keeps what, where and until; the rods set aside a second ago from here are not offered back, and from eight blocks off are, "set aside 10 seconds ago from (-104, 41, 108), 8 blocks from here"; a set-aside rung is not brought to its question and its budget does not run; leave_nether offers no search_on while the set-aside stands; the escalation carries when the first way below comes off rest. Each fails on the code before its change. Changed tests: tried.test.js's note 583 resume test (the rung's minutes were carried over; now the session's start afresh with the minutes before said), pearl-routes.test.js (the take-up's wording). npm test 1982 of 1982, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 59 of 61 with the two known gaps. Still open: nothing offers a walk of more than one cell among the unstuck moves (25592's missing move, not proven); digExposes reads the body's cells and the ring round them, not a dig two blocks off that lava could reach by flowing; the pathfinder's own moves are refused by movement.js's rules (note 580) and now also by the dig guard, which ends its path when it digs toward lava rather than planning round it; a crouched move walks back onto its footing only where the fall under the middle ends in lava; until_rest_ends and the set-aside's hold from here have not been probed against the live model; none of this has run live.

601. *Five wither skeleton deaths in an hour, the fortress cohort of 2026-09-28 (main at 87a6bdd, notes 571 to 599 live): mid-242-ah-fortress-1 (25587, 11:43:52), mid-242-ah-fortress-3 (25586, 12:13:05), mid-242-ah-nether-1 (25590, 12:14:36), mid-243-af-fortress-3 (25600, 12:26:10) and mid-242-af-nether-3-fortress-2 (25589, 12:36:34); a wither skeleton met as a player meets one was never offered.* All five in full iron with an iron sword and a shield. mid-242-ah-fortress-1, in a netherrack corner of a warped cavern with no pickaxe: eat was taken at 0.53 with the skeleton 8.5 blocks off, told "about 0 damage ... The mobs are all still here when it is done", and its blow landed 0.1 seconds after the meal, the shield down, 14.5 to 10; the fight chosen next swung once, knocked it back to 3.6, ran at it, the route came back partial, and the fight failed ("found no way to it") and was left out of the next asking (note 596's rule) with the skeleton walking back in; it ate again, then answered none of these at 0.40 (retreat 0.34), and the retreat stood 2.7 seconds searching for a way (wayAway, 24 route surveys) with the skeleton at arm's length and the shield down: two blows and the wither, 9.1 to 0. mid-242-ah-fortress-3: the fight's charge walked into a blow (20 to 15.5) and failed partial the same way; the retreat ran 16 blocks, and was held on past its run (holds.js, note 599: the skeleton, chosen against at 4.2, was 6.7 and then 2 blocks off, never four nearer), its run searched again standing, and two blows landed, 13.5 to 5.3, before encounter_stance was asked. mid-242-ah-nether-1: the same held retreat (struck 14.1 to 9.6 three seconds after its run), then leave_reach at 5.1 health with the skeleton at 0.6 blocks, told only "about 6 blocks behind when the run ends, and at the bot about 1.2 seconds after": it struck as the run began, 5.1 to 0.6, and the wither did the rest; neither run carried a damage figure for what follows. mid-243-af-fortress-3 ate at 6.4 health with the skeleton 6.4 blocks off, at arm's length 1.3 seconds into a 1.6-second meal, told "About 5 damage ... while it eats" (its blow whole and a fifth of a second of the wither): the blow took 4.8, then the fight, then dead. mid-242-af-nether-3-fortress-2: at 6.9 health the pillar was priced honestly as reached (the skeleton's ground three up, 15.7 from 6.9) and was taken as the best listed when Jev answered none of these at 0.43; the pillar did rise, 71 to 73, and the skeleton struck its top. From the 26.1.2 jar: a wither skeleton is 0.7 by 2.4 (EntityType sized) and its box is not let into a space under three blocks; its blow lands where its box widened sqrt(2.04) - 0.6 sideways and not up meets the player's (about 1.5 blocks centre to centre), where a player's sword reaches three from the eye; a shield raised facing a blow takes it whole, a hit taken whole is no hurt (LivingEntity.hurtServer returns false) and the wither is added only after a blow that hurts (WitherSkeleton.doHurtTarget), a stone sword disables no shield, a blocked blow knocks the attacker back, and its blows come each twenty ticks at reach with the arm's swing the client sees (MeleeAttackGoal, Mob.swing). Nothing offered either way: walk-reach searched every walker one block high, so a space two high was a way to the bot for a wither skeleton and the bunker priced it as following in; no stance met it with the shield between its blows. Now: walk-reach stands a walker only where its own height fits (ceil of BODY_HEIGHT; walkers of different heights searched each by its own) and takes cells to be dug or filled (`dug`, `placed`), saying the headroom where it keeps one off. src/wither-guard.js: bladeReaches (the jar's box), lowCeilingPlan (a ceiling two up over the bot's cell and each open cell round it, each block against a face, the cells toward the tall walkers first and its own last, or a hole two in and two high dug into rock with rock over it, the quicker; offered only where walk-reach then keeps every tall walker off and no body stands where a block goes), and guard (the shield up facing the biter at its reach or the one chosen against, the sword swung right after its arm swings or while it is within the sword's reach and out of its own, only while every biter at reach has just struck, no jump for a critical, the shield straight back up). Two stances: shield_guard (a shield carried and a biter, not a creeper, shooter or spear holder, that can get to the bot within sixteen; priced with the one faced blocking and the rest fought, said with the rules, the swings to kill and what the arena measured beside the fight as it was) and low_ceiling (a walker taller than two that can get to the bot within sixteen; priced with the setup's seconds under what gets there and the tall ones out of reach after, a race said and priced as the fight where one can be at the bot before the cells round it are in). The mechanics: the fight's run at a walker that walk-reach finds coming is held facing it with the shield up when the route comes back cut short, not failed; the route search of a retreat or leave_reach (wayAway) stands behind the shield facing a biter within four and lowers it for the run; a retreat or leave_reach is not held on past its run; a hold is asked again when a biter it was chosen against comes to arm's length from out of it (holds.diverged); a meal is priced with the wither and poison its blows leave counted whole (stanceCost effectsTo) and says a biter at arm's length within or up to one and a half seconds after it, with its blow and wither; retreat and leave_reach are priced with what follows (chaseCost: a blow as the bot turns where one is at its reach, blows through the run from one as fast as it, a blow on its arrival after the run within fifteen seconds, the wither after), in the figure and its expects. The drill (scripts/lib/arena.js): `wither_cave_corner`, a warped cavern with the bot in a netherrack corner, the death's kit (full iron, iron sword, shield, stone axe, no pickaxe, netherrack, mutton), from 14.5 health and hunger 16 (the Hunger effect cleared at sixteen, then the damage: left running it starved the first runs to 10), one wither skeleton 11 blocks off; `wither_cave_pair` the same with a second. Before (main's code), Jev's own choice, five runs each: one, 0 deaths, 5 of 5 killed, 2.8 damage on the median, 8.2 seconds, none of these answered in 3 runs (fight and eat chosen); two, 0 deaths, 10 of 10, 5.3, 10.2 seconds. Measured with ARENA_PREFER: shield_guard, one 0 damage in 5 of 5, 7.4 seconds; two 3.6, 9.1 seconds, no deaths; low_ceiling (the roof in the corner, 4 blocks), one 0 damage in 4 of 5 and a death (the skeleton under a cell before its block went in, then the hole dug by hand forced; the order now puts the cells toward it first and says the race), two 0 damage in 5 of 5, 12.2 seconds. After, Jev's own choice: shield_guard in all ten runs; one, 0 deaths, 5 of 5, 0 damage, 7.4 seconds; two, 0 deaths, 10 of 10, 3.5 damage, 10.6 seconds; no none of these. Probed against the live model, 5 each, the recorded questions of mid-242-ah-fortress-1 as recorded and as now asked (the recorded options with the new ones rendered on the saved ground at the recorded positions, the meal as now said): 11:43:42.6 eat 5 of 5 to shield_guard 5 of 5; 11:43:48.0 none of these 5 of 5 to shield_guard 5 of 5; 11:43:51.4 fight 3 and none of these 2 to shield_guard 5 of 5; replay case wither-skeleton-shield-guard-242ah. Tests (test/wither-guard.test.js, on the saved ground, test/fixtures/cavern-wither-25587.json): the ceiling keeps the skeleton off and a zombie not; the roof found for the recorded corner; the blade by the jar's box; the 11:43:42.6 question offers both, priced and measured, the race said, and the meal says the skeleton at the bot as it ends; the 11:43:45.5 meal priced with the wither whole; the guard's timing; the fight held with the shield when the run is cut short; the retreat's search behind the shield; a hold asked again at arm's length; a retreat not held on past its run; leave_reach at 0.6 blocks priced with the blow as it turns; mid-243-af-fortress-3's meal priced more than 6.4. Each fails on the old code but the blade's and the guard's, which test the new module. npm test and JEV_ARBITER=shadow npm test 1973 of 1973; replay suite 60 of 62 with the two known gaps. Still open: the fight's closing (PACE, leadFor) is still the old charge's, 13.6 seconds for a skeleton that walks in in 2.1, and prices the fight high against every other stance (notes 555, 559); why the charge's route comes back partial on warped ground at two to four blocks is not known (the fight now holds instead); an existing low ceiling within a few steps is not looked for, only one made where the bot stands or a step off; the guard faces one biter and waits on the rest, so a pair still lands blows (3.5 a run); the drill starts the skeleton eleven blocks off, and none of this has run live.

602. *close_in at a live spawner with four or more blazes, chosen in the fortress cohort's blaze deaths and closing on nothing: mid-243-ag-fortress-1 (25592, 11:45:42), mid-242-ah-nether-2-fortress-1 (25584, 11:58:42), mid-242-ah-nether-1-fortress-2 (25590, 12:28:27); and mid-242-ah-fortress-2 (25587, 11:50:31), burned to death on a flame it never put out.* mid-243-ag was asked its stance at 14.1 health, alight with seven seconds of fire left, four blazes nine to fifteen off in sight (eight about, a spawner twelve off). close_in said "About 21 damage a run, from 14.1 health (more than the bot has), as measured": the median of the arena's three runs against three blazes twenty to twenty-five off, not this fight; back_to_wall said 61.8 and take_cover 48.1, from the steady estimate (a blaze in sight priced at a fireball every two seconds and alight throughout). close_in was chosen (0.49), and the bot stood on one cell 22 seconds behind the shield, not a step nor a swing, the fire from a flame at 11:45:01 burning it to 7.1; chosen again at 7.1 (0.37), a fireball and its fire took it to 0.4, where the stance turned five ways in three seconds. The flight record says why it never walked: every walk toward a blaze was "no route". The hunt's claim that lets a walk near a shooter in sight (danger.js hunted) lapses under fourteen health (mob-policy fitToFight, note 577's open item), and at 13.1 every cell nearer a blaze in sight was refused (safeFromHostiles). mid-242-ah-nether-2-fortress-1 fell six blocks among eight blazes one to eight off by the spawner and chose close_in at 13 and 5.5 on the same 21: it swung (the sword lost nine durability in six seconds, nine hits), killed none, and five fireballs and the fire took it to none; the fight option beside it said 141. mid-242-ah-nether-1-fortress-2 took close_in at 1.1 (0.24, none_good 0.23) with a blaze at 0.9. mid-242-ah-fortress-2 stood at y 44 on the blocks it had risen on out of an earlier fire, a drop on every side, when a flame was lit in its cell at 11:50:21: fireWays found no run and no rise, so body_way was never asked, the old run found no steps twenty-one times a second ("[loop] spinning"), and it burned from 8.5 to none in ten seconds, the flame a punch away; the eat at the end was the last second. Why the shield timing (note 577) did not save them: a shield does not stop the burning already on the body or a flame stood in, which were most of the damage in both 25592 fights; the blazes were spread round the bot (at 11:45:32 five in sight from 17 to 84 degrees off the facing, three more behind), and the fireball that landed came from the edge of the half the shield covers; and in this note's drill 19 of the 28 fireballs that landed came with the shield raised a quarter second or more, 14 of them with a glowing blaze past 90 degrees. Now: close_in is priced by the sum of the fight at hand (blaze-stand.js closeInCost, closeInSays), the game's numbers blaze by blaze: each that sees the bot throws three about every nine seconds, each landing by its distance (fireballHit), a blaze within two swinging once a second instead; a landing is its hit through the armour worn and five seconds alight, the chance of being alight 1 - e^(-5λ); the shield faced at their middle covers those within sixty degrees of it at the probe's one in thirty, the rest land as if it were down; the walk goes only in the lulls, (1 - 2.2/8.9)^k of the time with k in sight; each blaze in reach takes the sword's swings at the fights' pace (four at 0.9) with the shield down while the rest shoot; the fire on the body burns on; a live spawner within sixteen puts in about one every six seconds up to six. The option says each part and what it comes to, and when the health runs out if it does ("the health runs out at about 9.9 seconds in, after about 1 of them killed" for mid-243-ag at 11:45:02; "about 1.9 seconds in, after about 0" for the eight at 11:58:35; one blaze at eight in the open, about 2.7 over 5.1 seconds and the blaze killed), its expects is that sum, and the arena's rows follow it, each said with its own fight. break_spawner is priced the same way, the walk to the cage and the digging first and no newcomers after (taken twice to death in the drill once close_in said its sum, on its own row's 34.1). dig_in_at_spawner is priced by its own cost, not the row. The other stances' blaze fire is at the game's pace too (combat-estimate.js blazeCadence: a landing about every 8.9 / (3 · fireballHit(d)) seconds, alight 1 - e^(-5/every) of the time), and the fires of several blazes burn as one fire lit by any of them (within: 1 - the product of each one not lighting it), where one blaze ten off was 1.35 a second and alight throughout. The walk of a stance that goes at the blazes may near them whatever the health (danger.js closingOn, set by blaze-stand.js claimBlazes for the close-in's time), not a claim the reflexes yield to. A close-in that gets nowhere for a volley's cycle (no swing, no rod, not a block nearer the blaze it goes for) ends and says why ("9 seconds without a step nearer a blaze or a swing; the shield up for volleys 7 of them, from 6 blazes in sight; the walk to one failed 7 times (No route ...); no ground within the sword's reach of 2 of them"), taken as a stance it fails with that (StanceFailed), and close_in is a striking stance (note 596). body_way's fire question offers put_out_flames, the flames the body stands in or beside punched out where it stands, a quarter second a flame. Drill (scripts/lib/arena.js blaze_spawner_four_near: the fortress arena, the bot 11.5 from the live spawner, four blazes nine to eleven off round it, two over the floor and two over the lava; ARENA_JEV=1, five runs; the arena session's item kill and end sweep are now kept to its own arenas, so another worktree's drills on the same server keep theirs): before (1879a71) 1 death, 2 rods, median damage 23.6, 10 strikes a run on the median, close_in chosen 3 times; the price alone (the walk still refused under fourteen) 2 deaths, 0 rods, 32.9, the walks "no route" 7 and 14 times in a stall; with the price, break_spawner's and the rows after 1 death, 2 rods, 42.2; all of it (the walk let near, the cadence) 2 deaths, 3 rods, 30.2, 9 strikes a run on the median (30 swings struck at blazes), the walk failing 6 times in all, close_in chosen in every run and a rod in three: it closes and strikes now, and a live spawner with four blazes is still lost two times in five with this kit. Probed against the live model, 5 each, the recorded texts against the same with close_in's text as now: 11:45:02 close_in 5 to close_in 2, back_to_wall 3; 11:58:35 close_in 5 to back_to_wall 3, eat 2 (added to the replay suite as close-in-eight-blazes-about-13-242ah, close_in forbidden); mid-243-af-nether-3-fortress-3's lone blaze at 12:22:24 and 12:22:33 take_cover 5 both as recorded, take_cover 4 and close_in 1 each now. Tests: blaze-stand.test.js, mid-243-ag's scene priced to the health's end with its figures said, one blaze at eight not; the eight about the bot dead before the first kill; the close-in with no route ends within its cycle, says why, fails as a stance, and its walk may near a blaze at 10 health (not after); break_spawner's sum; vitals.test.js, the flame on the risen column offered as put_out_flames only and punched; combat-estimate.test.js, a blaze's cadence and four blazes' one fire. Each fails without the change. npm test 1967 of 1967, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 60 of 62 with the two known gaps. Still open: why fireballs land through a shield raised and facing (note 577's, now counted: 19 of 28 in the drill); the stalk of a lone blaze (mid-243-af-nether-3-fortress-3, 12:22 to 12:23:40, one sword hit in ninety seconds) chose take_cover five times in five and is not looked at here; the other stances still use the halving shield and none of them says what the drill measured of the shield; the fight stance's run at a blaze in sight is still refused under fourteen (only the blaze stands set closingOn); none of this has run in a trial.

603. *Busy but not progressing in the cohort begun at 11:32Z (main at 87a6bdd): mid-244-be (25585) and mid-244-bd (25581), both fresh worlds, "flipping cast_portal <-> enter_nether" with nine of ten cast; mid-243-af-nether-3-fortress-1 (25591), "between steps" 10 of 19 minutes and then "No reachable surveyed ground while searching for crimson_stem"; mid-243-af-fortress-1 (25589) and mid-243-ag-fortress-3 (25592, from the same save), "tunnel <-> cross_toward" and then unstuck_move round and round; and mid-242-ae-nether-3-fortress-3 (25584), "find_fortress <-> cross_toward" and "find fortress and rods waiting".* The chains, read from the logs, the flight records, the archived goals and the saved worlds. (1) The cast (25585, 25581). The frames stood nine of ten cast (the region files: (80, 23, 147) along x with its top slot (82, 27, 147) open; (56, 75, 142) with (58, 79, 142) open). No stand beside the top slot could be walked to (every half-second survey failed, and the bot never moved: (82, 24, 145) from 12:04:12 to 12:05:31), so each pass made one (portal-cast.js make_stand, a floor under a cell that would do), returned "not done", and the next pass, finding the stand it had made no nearer, made another: (81, 26, 145), (82, 26, 149), (83, 26, 149), (83, 27, 148) ... on 25585, twenty on 25581 in three minutes. The ladder names the rung's step (enter_nether) every pass and the cast names its own, so the flip watch saw two steps trading the turn; its three stall questions on 25585 were each discarded as asked on a changed state ("Discarded changed-state decision" at 12:04:53, 12:05:07, 12:05:21), and on 25581 portal_method was asked five times and kept cast_at_lava, the way that had built this frame, whose kept answer goes on with the frame (work.js portalMethod). The cast's "done" and the ladder's were the same; what was missing was the cast saying it had failed. (2) The Nether's wood (25591). Its iron pickaxe wore out at the fortress with one oak log carried (the flight at 11:34:04). leave_nether's go_back took it to the staircase back, which needs a pickaxe (work.js tunnelToward), and the planner made the sticks and the pickaxe of oak: the planks group was filled with oak because what was carried of it was priced at nothing (knowledge.js chooseIngredient: estimate seeds a carried stack at 0, whatever is needed), so the second oak log was to be mined in the Nether. "No oak log in the nether" then set the rods aside, rung_elsewhere sent it to the Overworld for an oak log (an errand), and the errand's way there was the same staircase needing the same log: WrongDimension ninety times a minute, each dropping the step and sleeping a second, which is the "between steps" time, and the errand never rested because errandStage read no set-aside. When a crimson stem was planned at last (12:04:20, the log spent), the Overworld's walking search found no ground to walk to from its bridge at (-135, 74, -130) and failed at once; persist and the stall's answers put it back fifteen times in eighty seconds (none_good up to 0.93), the flip watch struck "turning between mine and persist" against survival:food_hunt_chosen, and set_aside_rung, chosen at 12:05:32 and 12:05:35, set aside the errand, which errandStage did not read. (3) The way back (25589, 25592). leave_nether's go_back (11:39:13; 12:25:33) led to the staircase toward the portal 329 blocks off, which paced three cells ((-230, 53, -226) to (-231, 53, -225); (-145, 60, 337) to (-145, 60, 339)), began a new round where it paced ("not gaining on it; starting round 2"), tried the crossing between rounds (0 blocks carried) and paced again: "turning between tunnel and cross toward". Then portal_way (none good 0.36), the rest held with no detours, and working free, where place_north put a gravel at the feet north and dig_north_feet dug it out, six times each at 11:43:40 to 11:43:50, every answer "a block was dug or placed" and so getting somewhere in the ledger (the archived goal's entries), so neither rested. (4) 25584 at 12:04:35: the legs each ended at once and their crossings with them, struck as survival:keep_working, the stance answered three seconds before (actionOf's recent survival action), and that stance set aside; at 12:07:08 rung_progress set the rods aside, leave_nether's search_on took them up again a second later, the fortress legs every way resting escalated to the rung, round again four times in a second, and search_on was offered each time as untried. (5) mid-244-bd's staircase to its frame (11:54:04 to 11:54:19, 12:13:34 to 12:15:05) dug a new step every few seconds under the ladder's enter_nether and was raised three times as "turning between enter nether and tunnel"; the answers ("differently") dropped the shaft. Now, in general. A stand the cast made for a slot is remembered on the frame (standsMade) and walked to, the whole walk with the tower allowed, not a half-second survey; not reached, the cast throws "Nowhere to stand to pour into the frame slot at (x, y, z): the stand made for it at (...) could not be walked to from (...) (why)", a site failure (with obsidian cast, portal_method is asked with it said), and no second stand is made for that slot; gone, the record is dropped and that is thrown. The planner prices one more of an ingredient whose carried stack is already spoken for at its cost without that stack (estimateWithout), so the Nether's second plank is crimson; the one oak log is still used. An errand set aside waits its rest like any rung (errandStage reads the set-aside). A block dug or placed counts as something done (stillness.js marked, repeats.js cameOf, the ledger) only in a cell not dug or placed in the last ten minutes, the stall watch's own rule, so a gravel put down and dug back is nothing done. Two steps trading the turn: a step and its own retry (persist) are one step; a block dug or placed in a new cell is getting somewhere, as the stall watch counts it; the work's flip is keyed to the work, not a stance a few seconds old; and the flip is the work's failure in the ledger, the step's entry written blocked and the answer the work was carrying out (tried.owner) marked come to nothing with the flip said and owed to its question (tried.escalate), so it rests at the second, while the stall's question is raised as before. A leave_nether go_back is not held while a failure is owed to leave_nether (netherLeaveHeld): it is asked again with it said. A staircase that paces its cells is set aside there (staircaseStalled), wherever the round began: a round begun in the cells it paced paced them again. Shown on the recorded states: the cast replayed on each saved world from the region files, the loop's own stands cleared (the log names them), the surveys and walks failing as they did, lava and water carried: before, 25585's frame made stands at (81, 26, 145), (82, 26, 149), (83, 26, 149), (83, 27, 148), (81, 26, 149) ..., ten passes, the order the log has, and 25581's twenty-one passes and stands; after, one stand each, and at the second pass the cast fails with the stand and the walk's "No path to the goal!" said. The planner with 25591's pockets at 11:34:04, the pickaxe gone: wooden and stone pickaxe plans use the oak log and mine crimson stems, nothing in another dimension (before: a second oak log mined in the Nether). 25589's recorded working-free answers through the ledger with the watch's marks: place_north once getting somewhere, then every dig_north_feet and place_north coming to nothing, both resting at the second with dig_down left (before: all twelve getting somewhere). 25584's 12:07 round through the flip watch and the ledger: search_on come to nothing at the first round and resting at the second (before: untried each time). mid-244-bd's staircase names with a new step dug between: no flip (before: raised). Tests: ruined-portal.test.js (a stand made and not reached is thrown with it said and no second made; one reached is poured from), planner.test.js (25591's pockets in the Nether), game-progress.test.js (the errand's set-aside read, and its rest over), trading-turns.test.js (the gravel put back; the staircase under enter_nether; mine and persist, and the flip keyed to the rung under keep_working; search_on and the held go_back); each fails without its change. Changed test: tunneling.test.js, pacing far from where the round began now rests the staircase rather than beginning a round there. npm test 1968 of 1968, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 59 of 61 with the two known gaps. Still open: the stall questions discarded as asked on a changed state (three of three on 25585, 12:16:19 on 25581), not looked into past the fingerprint; the cast has no way up to the top slot from the ground when no walk reaches a stand (a pillar in place, say); the Nether's stone pickaxe still plans stone mined; the search for a crimson stem is the Overworld's walking search, not the Nether's forest sweep, and failed at once from a bridge; a flip's owner is found only when the answer it carried out is still under way (on 25589 and 25592 go_back had been settled as getting somewhere by then, so there the flip goes to the stall's question as before); none of this has run live.
604. *Two creeper deaths in ten minutes, both after a retreat, main at 87a6bdd: mid-242-af-nether-3-fortress-1 (25586, "blown up", 12:08:17.57) and mid-242-af-nether-2-fortress-2 (25583, 12:06:57.93).* Read from the flight records and copies of the saved regions. Read again from the 26.1.2 server jar: SwellGoal's canUse is swellDir above 0 or the target within three (distanceToSqr under 9, feet to feet); its tick sets swellDir -1 when the target is past seven (distanceToSqr over 49) or out of sight (Sensing.hasLineOfSight), else +1; Creeper's maxSwell is 30 and explosionRadius 3; ServerExplosion.hurtEntities measures from the creeper's feet to the target's position against twice the power, six blocks, and a lit creeper past seven and three drops its swell goal and walks on (movement 0.25, the melee goal's modifier 1: about 2.7 blocks a second). The bot's own runs are not the sprint's 5.6: over 538 retreats with a way found in the flight records of 2026-09-26 to 28, the median straight-line distance from where the answer came was 3.8 blocks at one second, 5.8 at one and a half and 8 at two (the slower quarter 4.8 at one and a half). 25586: at 12:08:05 the way down (note 565) was asked with no mob at hand, and dig_down (0.91) dug the column of the bot's own tower from 20 to (-40, 14, -5), told only that "a walk goes on" there; a creeper was on the cave floor at (-38.7, 11, -4.1), heard at 9 blocks and never said. It ended 3.1 blocks from it; the bridge's own block was in the line between their eyes, so it stood unlit for five seconds (why the bot was at 3 blocks). encounter_stance at 12:08:10.9 (8.9 health): fight, pillar, seal, dig_down, retreat; retreat 0.81, "18 blocks to footing ... passing none of them, about 3.2 seconds at a run" and not a word of the fuse. The run went east along the one-wide bridge at about 1.2 blocks a second, into skeleton 4412's line (its arrow at 14.6, 8.9 to 6.9), and the way the pathfinder held then went down to the floor 3.7 from the creeper and back west past where it had stood, about 4 blocks from it; a navigation stall at 15.5. At 15.97 retreat again (0.95), "13 blocks ... passing none of them": the run left the bridge's side, took a second over the three-block drop and landed at (-34.6, 11, -3.5), 0.6 blocks from the creeper and in its sight; 2.15 blocks east in the next 0.4 seconds and it went off 2.75 blocks off, 6.9 to none. The way scouted before the question is vetted against every mob by its points (none nearer than two blocks here); the run then asked the pathfinder for its own way to that footing, which nothing vetted. No stance stayed where the bot was, over the creeper and out of its sight: block_creeper (note 534) offers staying behind a block only once held, and the dance is not offered with a drop at the feet. 25583: the food walk went west from (-189, 37, 68) toward a creeper out of sight at 13.5 blocks; at 12:06:55.0, 6.5 health and no armour, the creeper 7.1 blocks off walking in: fight (priced to go off 4.5 blocks off, 7, more than the bot has), bunker, seal, eat ("about 0 damage ... while it eats"), keep_working, retreat, which said "No way found yet: 2 of 24 spots ... tried before it moves, up to about 3 seconds standing still"; retreat 0.32 over eat 0.30. It stood searching at (-198.5, 37, 63.84) from 55.3 to 57.9 while the creeper walked from 6.1 blocks to 2.8, lit and went off. block_creeper was not offered: it looked only within seven blocks, and this one was 7.1. Now a run is walked in time against every creeper within ten blocks or lit (src/creeper-run.js): the way from the bot's feet through each step's middle at the runs' measured pace, the creeper a tick at a time by the rules above (standing while within three or lit, burning only within seven and in sight of the ray from its eyes to the bot's, blocks stopping it, burning back otherwise, walking on its own level straight at the bot and sliding along a wall past three), until the fuse it could light before the way ends is done: where the bot is past seven or out of its sight and when, or where it goes off, how far and the blast after the armour worn, at the median and at the slower quarter's 3.2 blocks a second. wayAway takes the first way on which no creeper goes off and otherwise the one whose blast is least; the retreat and leave_reach say what the way does against the fuse with the rule, and count the blast in their price. The route search and the run alike carry a step cost that rises inside seven blocks of each such creeper (the pathfinder's exclusionAreasStep, put back after), so a way out goes away from it first and the way run is the way priced. A run's search standing still stops where a creeper coming on would be within three (a quarter second short; a moment for one lit or within three), and the option says so and, for the rest of a search it still stands for, when the creeper lights and where it goes off. block_creeper looks within ten blocks, and a block already in the creeper's line, the ground's or one laid, is offered as staying behind it (the recorded bridge: "Stay behind the netherrack at (-40, 13, -5)", 0 damage). A meal (eat, the golden apple) says a creeper walking in: when it is within three and lights, the fuse left when the meal is done and the blast if the bot is still there. The way down says a creeper within ten of where each way ends: how far, whether it sees the bot there (the column dug counted open) and when it is within three at its walk. Tests (test/creeper-run.test.js, on copies of the saved regions, test/fixtures/creeper-bridge-25586.json and creeper-cave-25583.json, the crater put back as the flight shows it stood, with the pathfinder's own search over them, test/fixtures/saved-ground.js): a lit creeper outrun straight away and going off on a way round it at four; one within three out of sight stands unlit, and a way round the wall into its line lights it; the recorded second run, out of its sight on the bridge and lit as the bot drops beside it, goes off with the bot in the blast at both paces; the scout on the bridge passes over the recorded destination's way for one that does not light it, and with the first run's way (12:08:10.9, round where the creeper stood) the only one, takes it with its blast said and in its price; the steer prices the floor beside the creeper and not the bridge's far end, is on during the run's navigation and put back; the run at 12:06:55 whose every search fails gives up within its budget, not after 24, said on the option; standing a search's 3.3 seconds the creeper goes off, said; the way down of 12:08:05 says it ends 3.1 blocks from the creeper, out of its sight there; live, the question carries it; on the bridge at 12:08:10.9 staying behind the bridge is offered; at 12:06:55 the block is offered for the creeper at 7.1 and the meal says the fuse left. Each fails without the change but the first two (the new module's rules). Changed: survival.test.js's block option no longer asserts a line already stopped is nothing to offer. Probed against the live model, five each, as now asked (the recorded state, the changed options re-said by the code on the saved ground): 12:08:10.9 block_creeper 5 of 5 (0.74 to 0.79, retreat 0.19 to 0.24; recorded retreat 0.81); 12:08:15.97 retreat 5 of 5 (0.93 to 0.94), its way now one on which the creeper does not light, block_creeper 0.04; 12:06:55.0 block_creeper 5 of 5 (0.91 to 0.95; recorded retreat 0.32; with the new retreat and the old meal text and no block, eat 5 of 5 at 0.29 to 0.32, then fight 5 of 5 at 0.34 to 0.39 once the meal said its fuse); the way down at 12:08:05 dig_down 5 of 5 (0.87 to 0.91; out of its sight there, as it stood). Added to the replay suite: creeper-coming-on-block-242af (expect block_creeper, never eat or none_good), 5 of 5. npm test 1972 of 1972, the same under JEV_ARBITER=shadow; replay suite 60 of 62 with the two known gaps. Still open: the creeper's walk in the timeline is straight at the bot, sliding along walls, not its path (creeperWalk's A* is for the block option); the recorded second run was slower than the slower quarter (a second for its three-block drop), so a run priced clear at the median can still be caught; a mid-run re-path is steered but not walked again against the fuse; a sword's knockback that puts an unlit creeper back past three is not priced as delaying it; the way down has no way that waits on the top; the food walk that brought the bot to the creeper at 25583 is not asked about a creeper out of sight; none of this has run live.
